EXECUTIVE READING

先给结论，再进入源码

核心机制

一次用户请求可以连续让模型说、用工具、再说；但最多转 100 圈，避免无尽自言自语。

证据规模

28 条 finding / 28 处引用

主要边界

当前源码证据仍需部署级验证

适用建设

一次用户请求可以连续让模型说、用工具、再说；但最多转 100 圈，避免无尽自言自语。

值得借鉴

  • 一次用户请求可以连续让模型说、用工具、再说；但最多转 100 圈，避免无尽自言自语。
  • 开口前先整理历史、确认装得下、保证工具回执不被编辑器消息插队，然后才选本轮模型和工具箱。
  • 第一次怀疑绕圈会给模型一次纠偏机会，第二次还绕就停。

需要警惕

  • 当前源码证据仍需部署级验证
  • 当前源码证据仍需部署级验证
  • 当前源码证据仍需部署级验证

阅读方式

  • 先看固定提交，再看调用链
  • 事实、推断、风险分栏
  • 每条 finding 可回到源码
00 · METHOD

研究口径：不是 README 摘要

README POLICY

README 只定位入口和产品命名；实现结论必须回到运行时代码、契约或测试。

FACT POLICY

L1 运行代码优先，L2 类型/事件契约补足，L3/L4 交叉验证，L5 推断单独标记。

01 · TECHNICAL MAPS

五张逐张展开的源码证据图

架构、时序、能力、主循环和指令执行图都独占一段；节点来自源码 finding 与指令线索，点击节点可查看固定提交、行号和执行语义。

02 · COVERAGE MAP

覆盖维度与证据等级

架构与所有权verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

主递归 turn、模型路由、next-speaker、loop recovery、hook 与上限。

模型与上下文verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

ContentGenerator、Google OAuth/API key/Vertex/Gateway、流式重试。

模型与上下文verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

legacy compression 与 graph/pipeline context-management 两条路径。

工具注册与执行verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

Turn 工具提取、event-driven Scheduler、输出落盘/截断和取消。

安全、审批与权限verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

规则/安全检查/审批模式、环境净化和三平台真实沙箱。

连接器、插件与 MCPverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

MCP transports/OAuth/refresh/admin policy，扩展热装卸，GEMINI.md/JIT memory，skills 和 hooks。

协作与子 AgentverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

local/remote protocol、AgentSession event replay、子 Agent 独立 registry/scheduler。

观测、日志与回放verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

增量 JSONL、rewind/checkpoint、OTEL/GCP/file/console、规模与许可证。

  195  export const EDIT_DISPLAY_NAME = 'Edit';

  197  export const READ_FILE_DISPLAY_NAME = 'ReadFile';

  201  export const WEB_SEARCH_DISPLAY_NAME = 'GoogleSearch';

  204
  205  /**
  206   * Mapping of legacy tool names to their current names.
  207   * This ensures backward compatibility for user-defined policies, skills, and hooks.
  208   */
  209  export const TOOL_LEGACY_ALIASES: Record<string, string> = {
  210    // Add future renames here, e.g.:
  211    search_file_content: GREP_TOOL_NAME,
  212  };
  213
  214  /**
  215   * Returns all associated names for a tool (including legacy aliases and current name).
  216   * This ensures that if multiple legacy names point to the same tool, we consider all of them
  217   * for policy application.
  218   */
  219  export function getToolAliases(name: string): string[] {
  220    const aliases = new Set<string>([name]);
  221
  222    // Determine the canonical (current) name
  223    const canonicalName = TOOL_LEGACY_ALIASES[name] ?? name;
  224    aliases.add(canonicalName);
  225
  226    // Find all other legacy aliases that point to the same canonical name
  227    for (const [legacyName, currentName] of Object.entries(TOOL_LEGACY_ALIASES)) {
  228      if (currentName === canonicalName) {
  229        aliases.add(legacyName);
  230      }
  231    }
  232
  233    return Array.from(aliases);
  234  }
  235
  236  /** Prefix used for tools discovered via the tool DiscoveryCommand. */
  237  export const DISCOVERED_TOOL_PREFIX = 'discovered_tool_';
  238
  239  /**
  240   * List of all built-in tool names.
  241   */
  242  import {
  243    isMcpToolName,
  244    parseMcpToolName,
  245    MCP_TOOL_PREFIX,
  246  } from './mcp-tool.js';
  247
  248  export const ALL_BUILTIN_TOOL_NAMES = [
  249    GLOB_TOOL_NAME,
  250    WRITE_TODOS_TOOL_NAME,

  184  export const TRACKER_CREATE_TASK_TOOL_NAME = 'tracker_create_task';
  185  export const TRACKER_UPDATE_TASK_TOOL_NAME = 'tracker_update_task';
  186  export const TRACKER_GET_TASK_TOOL_NAME = 'tracker_get_task';
  187  export const TRACKER_LIST_TASKS_TOOL_NAME = 'tracker_list_tasks';
  188  export const TRACKER_ADD_DEPENDENCY_TOOL_NAME = 'tracker_add_dependency';
  189  export const TRACKER_VISUALIZE_TOOL_NAME = 'tracker_visualize';
  190
  191  export const AGENT_TOOL_NAME = 'invoke_agent';
  192
  193  // Tool Display Names
  194  export const WRITE_FILE_DISPLAY_NAME = 'WriteFile';
  195  export const EDIT_DISPLAY_NAME = 'Edit';
  196  export const ASK_USER_DISPLAY_NAME = 'Ask User';
  197  export const READ_FILE_DISPLAY_NAME = 'ReadFile';
  198  export const GLOB_DISPLAY_NAME = 'FindFiles';
  199  export const LS_DISPLAY_NAME = 'ReadFolder';
  200  export const GREP_DISPLAY_NAME = 'SearchText';
  201  export const WEB_SEARCH_DISPLAY_NAME = 'GoogleSearch';
  202  export const WEB_FETCH_DISPLAY_NAME = 'WebFetch';
  203  export const READ_MANY_FILES_DISPLAY_NAME = 'ReadManyFiles';
  204
  205  /**
  206   * Mapping of legacy tool names to their current names.
  207   * This ensures backward compatibility for user-defined policies, skills, and hooks.
  208   */
  209  export const TOOL_LEGACY_ALIASES: Record<string, string> = {
  210    // Add future renames here, e.g.:
  211    search_file_content: GREP_TOOL_NAME,
  212  };
  213
  214  /**
  215   * Returns all associated names for a tool (including legacy aliases and current name).
  216   * This ensures that if multiple legacy names point to the same tool, we consider all of them
  217   * for policy application.
  218   */
  219  export function getToolAliases(name: string): string[] {
  220    const aliases = new Set<string>([name]);
  221
  222    // Determine the canonical (current) name
  223    const canonicalName = TOOL_LEGACY_ALIASES[name] ?? name;
  224    aliases.add(canonicalName);
  225
  226    // Find all other legacy aliases that point to the same canonical name
  227    for (const [legacyName, currentName] of Object.entries(TOOL_LEGACY_ALIASES)) {
  228      if (currentName === canonicalName) {
  229        aliases.add(legacyName);
  230      }
  231    }
  232
  233    return Array.from(aliases);
  234  }
  235
  236  /** Prefix used for tools discovered via the tool DiscoveryCommand. */
  237  export const DISCOVERED_TOOL_PREFIX = 'discovered_tool_';
  238
  239  /**
  240   * List of all built-in tool names.
  241   */
  242  import {
  243    isMcpToolName,
  244    parseMcpToolName,
  245    MCP_TOOL_PREFIX,
  246  } from './mcp-tool.js';
  247

  201  export const WEB_SEARCH_DISPLAY_NAME = 'GoogleSearch';
  202  export const WEB_FETCH_DISPLAY_NAME = 'WebFetch';
  203  export const READ_MANY_FILES_DISPLAY_NAME = 'ReadManyFiles';
  204
  205  /**
  206   * Mapping of legacy tool names to their current names.
  207   * This ensures backward compatibility for user-defined policies, skills, and hooks.
  208   */
  209  export const TOOL_LEGACY_ALIASES: Record<string, string> = {
  210    // Add future renames here, e.g.:
  211    search_file_content: GREP_TOOL_NAME,
  212  };
  213
  214  /**
  215   * Returns all associated names for a tool (including legacy aliases and current name).
  216   * This ensures that if multiple legacy names point to the same tool, we consider all of them
  217   * for policy application.
  218   */
  219  export function getToolAliases(name: string): string[] {
  220    const aliases = new Set<string>([name]);
  221
  222    // Determine the canonical (current) name
  223    const canonicalName = TOOL_LEGACY_ALIASES[name] ?? name;
  224    aliases.add(canonicalName);
  225
  226    // Find all other legacy aliases that point to the same canonical name
  227    for (const [legacyName, currentName] of Object.entries(TOOL_LEGACY_ALIASES)) {
  228      if (currentName === canonicalName) {
  229        aliases.add(legacyName);
  230      }
  231    }
  232
  233    return Array.from(aliases);
  234  }
  235
  236  /** Prefix used for tools discovered via the tool DiscoveryCommand. */
  237  export const DISCOVERED_TOOL_PREFIX = 'discovered_tool_';
  238
  239  /**
  240   * List of all built-in tool names.
  241   */

  243    isMcpToolName,

  245    MCP_TOOL_PREFIX,

   21    readonly description = 'Show version and environment info';
   22
   23    async execute(
   24      context: CommandContext,
   25      _args: string[] = [],
   26    ): Promise<CommandExecutionResponse> {
   27      const osVersion = process.platform;
   28      let sandboxEnv = 'no sandbox';
   29      if (process.env['SANDBOX'] && process.env['SANDBOX'] !== 'sandbox-exec') {
   30        sandboxEnv = process.env['SANDBOX'];
   31      } else if (process.env['SANDBOX'] === 'sandbox-exec') {
   32        sandboxEnv = `sandbox-exec (${
   33          process.env['SEATBELT_PROFILE'] || 'unknown'
   34        })`;
   35      }
   36      const modelVersion = context.agentContext.config.getModel() || 'Unknown';
   37      const cliVersion = await getVersion();
   38      const selectedAuthType =
   39        context.settings.merged?.security?.auth?.selectedType ?? '';
   40      const gcpProject = process.env['GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT'] || '';
   41      const ideClient = await getIdeClientName(context);
   42
   43      const userAccountManager = new UserAccountManager();
   44      const cachedAccount = userAccountManager.getCachedGoogleAccount();
   45      const userEmail = cachedAccount ?? 'Unknown';
   46
   47      const tier = context.agentContext.config.getUserTierName() || 'Unknown';
   48
   49      const info = [
   50        `- Version: ${cliVersion}`,
   51        `- OS: ${osVersion}`,
   52        `- Sandbox: ${sandboxEnv}`,
   53        `- Model: ${modelVersion}`,
   54        `- Auth Type: ${selectedAuthType}`,
   55        `- GCP Project: ${gcpProject}`,
   56        `- IDE Client: ${ideClient}`,
   57        `- User Email: ${userEmail}`,
   58        `- Tier: ${tier}`,
   59      ].join('\n');
   60
   61      return {
   62        name: this.name,
   63        data: `Gemini CLI Info:\n${info}`,
   64      };
   65    }
   66  }
   67
   68  async function getIdeClientName(context: CommandContext) {
   69    if (!context.agentContext.config.getIdeMode()) {
   70      return '';
   71    }
   72    const ideClient = await IdeClient.getInstance();
   73    return ideClient?.getDetectedIdeDisplayName() ?? '';
   74  }

gemini

CLI 子命令 · named CLI command · packages/cli/src/utils/sandbox.ts:749
源码 ↗

container's /etc/passwd file, which is required by os.userInfo().

gemini→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  744        // Instead of passing --user to the main sandbox container, we let it
  745        // start as root, then create a user with the host's UID/GID, and
  746        // finally switch to that user to run the gemini process. This is
  747        // necessary on Linux to ensure the user exists within the
  748        // container's /etc/passwd file, which is required by os.userInfo().
  749        const username = 'gemini';
  750        const homeDir = getContainerPath(homedir());
  751        const quotedHomeDir = quote([homeDir]);
  752
  753        const originalCommand = finalEntrypoint[2];
  754        const escapedOriginalCommand = originalCommand.replace(/'/g, "'\\''");
  755
  756        // Use defensive entrypoint logic that checks for useradd availability.
  757        // This ensures we can support UID/GID mapping on distros that have these
  758        // tools. If useradd is missing (e.g. on minimal images), we fail explicitly
  759        // to avoid insecurely falling back to root execution with host mounts.
  760        const defensiveEntrypoint = [
  761          `if command -v useradd >/dev/null 2>&1; then`,
  762          `  (groupadd -g ${gid} -o ${username} 2>/dev/null || true) &&`,
  763          `  (id ${uid} >/dev/null 2>&1 || useradd -o -u ${uid} -g ${gid} -d ${quotedHomeDir} -s /bin/bash ${username} 2>/dev/null || true) &&`,
  764          `  USER_NAME=$(id -nu ${uid} 2>/dev/null);`,
  765          `  if [ -n "$USER_NAME" ]; then`,
  766          `    su -p "$USER_NAME" -c '${escapedOriginalCommand}';`,
  767          `  else`,
  768          `    echo "Error: Failed to map host UID ${uid} to a user in the container." >&2;`,
  769          `    exit 1;`,
  770          `  fi`,
  771          `else`,
  772          `  echo "Error: 'useradd' not found in container. UID/GID mapping is required for Linux distros like NixOS/Arch to avoid permission issues. Please use a container image that includes standard user management tools (like 'ubuntu' or 'debian')." >&2;`,
  773          `  exit 1;`,
  774          `fi`,
  775        ].join('\n');
  776
  777        // The entrypoint is always `['bash', '-c', '<command>']`, so we modify the command part.
  778        finalEntrypoint[2] = defensiveEntrypoint;
  779
  780        // We still need userFlag for the simpler proxy container, which does not have this issue.
  781        userFlag = `--user ${uid}:${gid}`;
  782        // When forcing a UID in the sandbox, $HOME can be reset to '/', so we copy $HOME as well.
  783        args.push('--env', `HOME=${homedir()}`);
  784      }
  785
  786      // push container image name
  787      args.push(image);
  788
  789      // push container entrypoint (including args)
  790      args.push(...finalEntrypoint);
  791
  792      // start and set up proxy if GEMINI_SANDBOX_PROXY_COMMAND is set
  793      let proxyProcess: ChildProcess | undefined = undefined;
  794      let sandboxProcess: ChildProcess | undefined = undefined;
  795
  796      if (proxyCommand) {
  797        // run proxyCommand in its own container
  798        // build args array to prevent command injection
  799        const proxyContainerArgs = [
  800          'run',
  801          '--rm',
  802          '--init',
  803          '--entrypoint',
  804          '',
  805          ...(userFlag ? userFlag.split(' ') : []),
  806          '--name',
  807          SANDBOX_PROXY_NAME,
  808          '--network',
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

   13
   14  export class HelpCommand implements Command {
   15    readonly name = 'help';
   16    readonly description = 'Show available commands';
   17
   18    constructor(private registry: CommandRegistry) {}
   19
   20    async execute(
   21      _context: CommandContext,
   22      _args: string[] = [],
   23    ): Promise<CommandExecutionResponse> {
   24      const commands = this.registry
   25        .getAllCommands()
   26        .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
   27
   28      const lines: string[] = [];
   29
   30      lines.push('Gemini CLI Help:');
   31      lines.push('');
   32      lines.push('### Basics');
   33      lines.push(
   34        '- **Add context**: Use `@` to specify files for context (e.g., `@src/myFile.ts`) to target specific files or folders.',
   35      );
   36      lines.push('');
   37
   38      lines.push('### Commands');
   39      for (const cmd of commands) {
   40        if (cmd.description) {
   41          lines.push(`- **/${cmd.name}** - ${cmd.description}`);
   42        }
   43      }
   44
   45      return {
   46        name: this.name,
   47        data: lines.join('\n'),
   48      };
   49    }
   50  }

/clear→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
   14  import { CommandKind, type SlashCommand } from './types.js';
   15  import { MessageType } from '../types.js';
   16  import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
   17
   18  export const clearCommand: SlashCommand = {
   19    name: 'clear',
   20    altNames: ['new'],
   21    description: 'Clear the screen and start a new session',
   22    kind: CommandKind.BUILT_IN,
   23    autoExecute: true,
   24    action: async (context, _args) => {
   25      const geminiClient = context.services.agentContext?.geminiClient;
   26      const config = context.services.agentContext?.config;
   27
   28      // Fire SessionEnd hook before clearing
   29      const hookSystem = config?.getHookSystem();
   30      if (hookSystem) {
   31        await hookSystem.fireSessionEndEvent(SessionEndReason.Clear);
   32      }
   33
   34      // Reset user steering hints
   35      config?.injectionService.clear();
   36
   37      // Start a new conversation recording with a new session ID
   38      // We MUST do this before calling resetChat() so the new ChatRecordingService
   39      // initialized by GeminiChat picks up the new session ID.
   40      let newSessionId: string | undefined;
   41      if (config) {
   42        newSessionId = randomUUID();
   43        config.resetNewSessionState(newSessionId);
   44      }
   45
   46      if (geminiClient) {
   47        context.ui.setDebugMessage('Clearing terminal and resetting chat.');
   48
   49        // Close persistent browser sessions before resetting chat
   50        await resetBrowserSession();
   51
   52        // If resetChat fails, the exception will propagate and halt the command,
   53        // which is the correct behavior to signal a failure to the user.
   54        await geminiClient.resetChat();
   55      } else {
   56        context.ui.setDebugMessage('Clearing terminal.');
   57      }
   58
   59      // Fire SessionStart hook after clearing
   60      let result;
   61      if (hookSystem) {
   62        result = await hookSystem.fireSessionStartEvent(SessionStartSource.Clear);
   63      }
   64
   65      // Give the event loop a chance to process any pending telemetry operations
   66      // This ensures logger.emit() calls have fully propagated to the BatchLogRecordProcessor
   67      await new Promise((resolve) => setImmediate(resolve));
   68
   69      // Flush telemetry to ensure hooks are written to disk immediately
   70      // This is critical for tests and environments with I/O latency
   71      if (config) {
   72        await flushTelemetry(config);
   73      }
   74
   75      uiTelemetryService.clear(newSessionId);
   76      context.ui.clear();
   77
   78      if (result?.systemMessage) {

交互 slash 指令 · named command catalog · packages/cli/src/ui/commands/copyCommand.ts:16
源码 ↗

Copy the last result or code snippet to clipboard

/copy→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
   11    type SlashCommand,
   12    type SlashCommandActionReturn,
   13  } from './types.js';
   14
   15  export const copyCommand: SlashCommand = {
   16    name: 'copy',
   17    description: 'Copy the last result or code snippet to clipboard',
   18    kind: CommandKind.BUILT_IN,
   19    autoExecute: true,
   20    action: async (context, _args): Promise<SlashCommandActionReturn | void> => {
   21      const chat = context.services.agentContext?.geminiClient?.getChat();
   22      const history = chat?.getHistory();
   23
   24      // Get the last message from the AI (model role)
   25      const lastAiMessage = history
   26        ? history.filter((item) => item.role === 'model').pop()
   27        : undefined;
   28
   29      if (!lastAiMessage) {
   30        return {
   31          type: 'message',
   32          messageType: 'info',
   33          content: 'No output in history',
   34        };
   35      }
   36      // Extract text from the parts
   37      const lastAiOutput = lastAiMessage.parts
   38        ?.filter((part) => part.text)
   39        .map((part) => part.text)
   40        .join('');
   41
   42      if (lastAiOutput) {
   43        try {
   44          const settings = context.services.settings.merged;
   45          await copyToClipboard(lastAiOutput, settings);
   46
   47          return {
   48            type: 'message',
   49            messageType: 'info',
   50            content: 'Last output copied to the clipboard',
   51          };
   52        } catch (error) {
   53          const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
   54          debugLogger.debug(message);
   55
   56          return {
   57            type: 'message',
   58            messageType: 'error',
   59            content: `Failed to copy to the clipboard. ${message}`,
   60          };
   61        }
   62      } else {
   63        return {
   64          type: 'message',
   65          messageType: 'info',
   66          content: 'Last AI output contains no text to copy.',
   67        };
   68      }
   69    },
   70  };
   71

  244    kind: CommandKind.BUILT_IN,
  245    autoExecute: true,
  246    action: async (context, args): Promise<MessageActionReturn> => {
  247      const tag = args.trim();
  248      if (!tag) {
  249        return {
  250          type: 'message',
  251          messageType: 'error',
  252          content: 'Missing tag. Usage: /resume delete <tag>',
  253        };
  254      }
  255
  256      const { logger } = context.services;
  257      await logger.initialize();
  258      const deleted = await logger.deleteCheckpoint(tag);
  259
  260      if (deleted) {
  261        return {
  262          type: 'message',
  263          messageType: 'info',
  264          content: `Conversation checkpoint '${decodeTagName(tag)}' has been deleted.`,
  265        };
  266      } else {
  267        return {
  268          type: 'message',
  269          messageType: 'error',
  270          content: `Error: No checkpoint found with tag '${decodeTagName(tag)}'.`,
  271        };
  272      }
  273    },
  274    completion: async (context, partialArg) => {
  275      const chatDetails = await getSavedChatTags(context, true);
  276      return chatDetails
  277        .map((chat) => chat.name)
  278        .filter((name) => name.startsWith(partialArg));
  279    },
  280  };

Manage workspace directories

   79    }

   81
   82  export const directoryCommand: SlashCommand = {
   83    name: 'directory',
   84    altNames: ['dir'],
   85    description: 'Manage workspace directories',
   86    kind: CommandKind.BUILT_IN,
   87    subCommands: [
   88      {
   89        name: 'add',
   90        description:
   91          'Add directories to the workspace. Use comma to separate multiple paths',
   92        kind: CommandKind.BUILT_IN,
   93        autoExecute: false,
   94        showCompletionLoading: false,
   95        completion: async (context: CommandContext, partialArg: string) => {
   96          // Support multiple paths separated by commas
   97          const parts = partialArg.split(',');
   98          const lastPart = parts[parts.length - 1];
   99          const leadingWhitespace = lastPart.match(/^\s*/)?.[0] ?? '';
  100          const trimmedLastPart = lastPart.trimStart();
  101
  102          if (trimmedLastPart === '') {
  103            return [];
  104          }
  105
  106          const suggestions = await getDirectorySuggestions(trimmedLastPart);
  107
  108          // Filter out existing directories
  109          let filteredSuggestions = suggestions;
  110          if (context.services.agentContext?.config) {
  111            const workspaceContext =
  112              context.services.agentContext.config.getWorkspaceContext();
  113            const existingDirs = new Set(
  114              workspaceContext.getDirectories().map((dir) => path.resolve(dir)),
  115            );
  116
  117            filteredSuggestions = suggestions.filter((s) => {
  118              const expanded = expandHomeDir(s);
  119              const absolute = path.resolve(expanded);
  120
  121              if (existingDirs.has(absolute)) {
  122                return false;
  123              }
  124              if (
  125                absolute.endsWith(path.sep) &&
  126                existingDirs.has(absolute.slice(0, -1))
  127              ) {
  128                return false;
  129              }
  130              return true;
  131            });
  132          }
  133
  134          if (parts.length > 1) {
  135            const prefix = parts.slice(0, -1).join(',') + ',';
  136            return filteredSuggestions.map((s) => prefix + leadingWhitespace + s);
  137          }
  138
  139          return filteredSuggestions.map((s) => leadingWhitespace + s);
  140        },
  141        action: async (context: CommandContext, args: string) => {
  142          const {
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

源码 ↗

Analyzes the project and creates a tailored GEMINI.md file

/init→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
   13  } from './types.js';
   14  import { CommandKind } from './types.js';
   15  import { performInit } from '@google/gemini-cli-core';
   16
   17  export const initCommand: SlashCommand = {
   18    name: 'init',
   19    description: 'Analyzes the project and creates a tailored GEMINI.md file',
   20    kind: CommandKind.BUILT_IN,
   21    autoExecute: true,
   22    action: async (
   23      context: CommandContext,
   24      _args: string,
   25    ): Promise<SlashCommandActionReturn> => {
   26      if (!context.services.agentContext?.config) {
   27        return {
   28          type: 'message',
   29          messageType: 'error',
   30          content: 'Configuration not available.',
   31        };
   32      }
   33      const targetDir = context.services.agentContext.config.getTargetDir();
   34      const geminiMdPath = path.join(targetDir, 'GEMINI.md');
   35
   36      const result = performInit(fs.existsSync(geminiMdPath));
   37
   38      if (result.type === 'submit_prompt') {
   39        // Create an empty GEMINI.md file
   40        fs.writeFileSync(geminiMdPath, '', 'utf8');
   41
   42        context.ui.addItem(
   43          {
   44            type: 'info',
   45            text: 'Empty GEMINI.md created. Now analyzing the project to populate it.',
   46          },
   47          Date.now(),
   48        );
   49      }
   50
   51      // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unsafe-type-assertion
   52      return result as SlashCommandActionReturn;
   53    },
   54  };
   55

  335      const agentRegistry = config?.getAgentRegistry();

  338          type: 'message',

  342      }

  347      });
  348

  350
  351      let content =
  352        summary.errors.length > 0
  353          ? 'Agents reloaded with errors:'
  354          : 'Agents reloaded successfully:';
  355      content += `\n- Total: ${summary.totalLoaded} (${summary.localCount} local, ${summary.remoteCount} remote)`;
  356
  357      if (summary.newAgents.length > 0) {
  358        content += `\n- New: ${summary.newAgents.join(', ')}`;
  359      }
  360      if (summary.updatedAgents.length > 0) {
  361        content += `\n- Updated: ${summary.updatedAgents.join(', ')}`;
  362      }
  363      if (summary.deletedAgents.length > 0) {
  364        content += `\n- Deleted: ${summary.deletedAgents.join(', ')}`;
  365      }
  366      if (summary.errors.length > 0) {
  367        content += `\n- Errors: ${summary.errors.length} encountered during reload`;
  368      }
  369
  370      content += '\n\nRun /agents list to see all available agents.';
  371
  372      return {
  373        type: 'message',
  374        messageType: 'info',
  375        content,
  376      };
  377    },

  168      const tag = args.trim();

  170        return {
  171          type: 'message',
  172          messageType: 'error',
  173          content: 'Missing tag. Usage: /resume resume <tag>',
  174        };
  175      }
  176
  177      const { logger } = context.services;
  178      const config = context.services.agentContext?.config;
  179      await logger.initialize();
  180      const checkpoint = await logger.loadCheckpoint(tag);
  181      const conversation = checkpoint.history;
  182
  183      if (conversation.length === 0) {
  184        return {
  185          type: 'message',
  186          messageType: 'info',
  187          content: `No saved checkpoint found with tag: ${decodeTagName(tag)}.`,
  188        };
  189      }
  190
  191      const currentAuthType = config?.getContentGeneratorConfig()?.authType;
  192      if (
  193        checkpoint.authType &&
  194        currentAuthType &&
  195        checkpoint.authType !== currentAuthType
  196      ) {
  197        return {
  198          type: 'message',
  199          messageType: 'error',
  200          content: `Cannot resume chat. It was saved with a different authentication method (${checkpoint.authType}) than the current one (${currentAuthType}).`,
  201        };
  202      }
  203
  204      const rolemap: { [key: string]: MessageType } = {
  205        user: MessageType.USER,
  206        model: MessageType.GEMINI,
  207      };
  208
  209      const uiHistory: HistoryItemWithoutId[] = [];
  210
  211      for (const item of conversation.slice(INITIAL_HISTORY_LENGTH)) {
  212        const text =
  213          item.parts
  214            ?.filter((m) => !!m.text)
  215            .map((m) => m.text)
  216            .join('') || '';
  217        if (!text) {
  218          continue;
  219        }

   95    description: 'Jump back to a specific message and restart the conversation',
   96    kind: CommandKind.BUILT_IN,
   97    action: (context) => {
   98      const agentContext = context.services.agentContext;
   99      const config = agentContext?.config;
  100      if (!config)
  101        return {
  102          type: 'message',
  103          messageType: 'error',
  104          content: 'Config not found',
  105        };
  106
  107      const client = agentContext.geminiClient;
  108      if (!client)
  109        return {
  110          type: 'message',
  111          messageType: 'error',
  112          content: 'Client not initialized',
  113        };
  114
  115      const recordingService = client.getChatRecordingService();
  116      if (!recordingService)
  117        return {
  118          type: 'message',
  119          messageType: 'error',
  120          content: 'Recording service unavailable',
  121        };
  122
  123      const conversation = recordingService.getConversation();
  124      if (!conversation)
  125        return {
  126          type: 'message',
  127          messageType: 'info',
  128          content: 'No conversation found.',
  129        };
  130
  131      const hasUserInteractions = conversation.messages.some(
  132        (msg) => msg.type === 'user',
  133      );
  134      if (!hasUserInteractions) {
  135        return {
  136          type: 'message',
  137          messageType: 'info',
  138          content: 'Nothing to rewind to.',
  139        };
  140      }
  141
  142      return {
  143        type: 'custom_dialog',
  144        component: (
  145          <RewindViewer
  146            conversation={conversation}
  147            onExit={() => {
  148              context.ui.removeComponent();
  149            }}
  150            onRewind={async (messageId, newText, outcome) => {
  151              if (outcome !== RewindOutcome.Cancel) {
  152                logRewind(config, new RewindEvent(outcome));
  153              }

  210  export const setupGithubCommand: SlashCommand = {
  211    name: 'setup-github',
  212    description: 'Set up GitHub Actions',
  213    kind: CommandKind.BUILT_IN,
  214    autoExecute: true,
  215    action: async (
  216      context: CommandContext,
  217    ): Promise<SlashCommandActionReturn> => {
  218      if (!isGitHubRepository()) {
  219        throw new Error(
  220          'Unable to determine the GitHub repository. /setup-github must be run from a git repository.',
  221        );
  222      }
  223
  224      // Find the root directory of the repo
  225      let gitRepoRoot: string;
  226      try {
  227        gitRepoRoot = getGitRepoRoot();
  228      } catch (error) {
  229        debugLogger.debug(`Failed to get git repo root:`, error);
  230        throw new Error(
  231          'Unable to determine the GitHub repository. /setup-github must be run from a git repository.',
  232        );
  233      }
  234
  235      // Get the latest release tag from GitHub
  236      const proxy = context?.services?.agentContext?.config.getProxy();
  237      const releaseTag = await getLatestGitHubRelease(proxy);
  238      const readmeUrl = `https://github.com/google-github-actions/run-gemini-cli/blob/${releaseTag}/README.md#quick-start`;
  239
  240      // Create workflows directory
  241      const workflowsDir = path.join(gitRepoRoot, '.github', 'workflows');
  242      await createDirectory(workflowsDir);
  243
  244      // Create commands directory
  245      const commandsDir = path.join(gitRepoRoot, '.github', 'commands');
  246      await createDirectory(commandsDir);
  247
  248      await downloadSetupFiles({
  249        configs: [
  250          { paths: GITHUB_WORKFLOW_PATHS, targetDir: workflowsDir },
  251          { paths: GITHUB_COMMANDS_PATHS, targetDir: commandsDir },
  252        ],
  253        releaseTag,
  254        proxy,
  255      });
  256
  257      // Add entries to .gitignore file
  258      await updateGitignore(gitRepoRoot);
  259
  260      // Print out a message
  261      const commands = [];
  262      if (process.platform !== 'win32') {
  263        commands.push('set -eEuo pipefail');
  264      }
  265      commands.push(
  266        `echo "Successfully downloaded ${GITHUB_WORKFLOW_PATHS.length} workflows , ${GITHUB_COMMANDS_PATHS.length} commands and updated .gitignore. Follow the steps in ${readmeUrl} (skipping the /setup-github step) to complete setup."`,
  267      );
  268      commands.push(...getOpenUrlsCommands(readmeUrl));
  269
  270      const command = `(${commands.join(' && ')})`;

/share

交互 slash 指令 · named command catalog · packages/cli/src/ui/commands/chatCommand.ts:283
源码 ↗

description:

/share→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  278        .filter((name) => name.startsWith(partialArg));
  279    },
  280  };
  281
  282  const shareCommand: SlashCommand = {
  283    name: 'share',
  284    description:
  285      'Share the current conversation to a markdown or json file. Usage: /resume share <file>',
  286    kind: CommandKind.BUILT_IN,
  287    autoExecute: false,
  288    action: async (context, args): Promise<MessageActionReturn> => {
  289      let filePathArg = args.trim();
  290      if (!filePathArg) {
  291        filePathArg = `gemini-conversation-${Date.now()}.json`;
  292      }
  293
  294      const filePath = path.resolve(filePathArg);
  295      const extension = path.extname(filePath);
  296      if (extension !== '.md' && extension !== '.json') {
  297        return {
  298          type: 'message',
  299          messageType: 'error',
  300          content: 'Invalid file format. Only .md and .json are supported.',
  301        };
  302      }
  303
  304      const chat = context.services.agentContext?.geminiClient?.getChat();
  305      if (!chat) {
  306        return {
  307          type: 'message',
  308          messageType: 'error',
  309          content: 'No chat client available to share conversation.',
  310        };
  311      }
  312
  313      const history = chat.getHistory();
  314
  315      // An empty conversation has a hidden message that sets up the context for
  316      // the chat. Thus, to check whether a conversation has been started, we
  317      // can't check for length 0.
  318      if (history.length <= INITIAL_HISTORY_LENGTH) {
  319        return {
  320          type: 'message',
  321          messageType: 'info',
  322          content: 'No conversation found to share.',
  323        };
  324      }
  325
  326      try {
  327        await exportHistoryToFile({ history, filePath });
  328        return {
  329          type: 'message',
  330          messageType: 'info',
  331          content: `Conversation shared to ${filePath}`,
  332        };
  333      } catch (err) {
  334        const errorMessage = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
  335        return {
  336          type: 'message',
  337          messageType: 'error',
  338          content: `Error sharing conversation: ${errorMessage}`,
  339        };
  340      }
  341    },
  342  };

/trust

交互 slash 指令 · named command catalog · packages/cli/src/ui/commands/permissionsCommand.ts:25
源码 ↗

Manage folder trust settings and other permissions

/trust→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
   20    description: 'Manage folder trust settings and other permissions',
   21    kind: CommandKind.BUILT_IN,
   22    autoExecute: false,
   23    subCommands: [
   24      {
   25        name: 'trust',
   26        description:
   27          'Manage folder trust settings. Usage: /permissions trust [<directory-path>]',
   28        kind: CommandKind.BUILT_IN,
   29        autoExecute: false,
   30        action: (context, input): SlashCommandActionReturn => {
   31          const dirPath = input.trim();
   32          let targetDirectory: string;
   33
   34          if (!dirPath) {
   35            targetDirectory = process.cwd();
   36          } else {
   37            targetDirectory = path.resolve(expandHomeDir(dirPath));
   38          }
   39
   40          try {
   41            if (!fs.statSync(targetDirectory).isDirectory()) {
   42              return {
   43                type: 'message',
   44                messageType: 'error',
   45                content: `Path is not a directory: ${targetDirectory}`,
   46              };
   47            }
   48          } catch (e) {
   49            const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
   50            return {
   51              type: 'message',
   52              messageType: 'error',
   53              content: `Error accessing path: ${targetDirectory}. ${message}`,
   54            };
   55          }
   56
   57          return {
   58            type: 'dialog',
   59            dialog: 'permissions',
   60            props: {
   61              targetDirectory,
   62            },
   63          } as OpenDialogActionReturn;
   64        },
   65      },
   66    ],
   67    action: (context, input): SlashCommandActionReturn => {

   16  /**
   17   * Command to open the upgrade page for Gemini Code Assist.
   18   * Only intended to be shown/available when the user is logged in with Google.
   19   */
   20  export const upgradeCommand: SlashCommand = {
   21    name: 'upgrade',
   22    kind: CommandKind.BUILT_IN,
   23    description: 'Upgrade your Gemini Code Assist tier for higher limits',
   24    autoExecute: true,
   25    action: async (context) => {
   26      const config = context.services.agentContext?.config;
   27      const authType = config?.getContentGeneratorConfig()?.authType;
   28      if (authType !== AuthType.LOGIN_WITH_GOOGLE) {
   29        // This command should ideally be hidden if not logged in with Google,
   30        // but we add a safety check here just in case.
   31        return {
   32          type: 'message',
   33          messageType: 'error',
   34          content:
   35            'The /upgrade command is only available when logged in with Google.',
   36        };
   37      }
   38
   39      const tierName = config?.getUserTierName();
   40      if (isUltraTier(tierName)) {
   41        return {
   42          type: 'message',
   43          messageType: 'info',
   44          content: `You are already on the highest tier: ${tierName}.`,
   45        };
   46      }
   47
   48      if (!shouldLaunchBrowser()) {
   49        return {
   50          type: 'message',
   51          messageType: 'info',
   52          content: `Please open this URL in a browser: ${UPGRADE_URL_PAGE}`,
   53        };
   54      }
   55
   56      try {
   57        await openBrowserSecurely(UPGRADE_URL_PAGE);
   58      } catch (error) {
   59        return {
   60          type: 'message',
   61          messageType: 'error',
   62          content: `Failed to open upgrade page: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
   63        };
   64      }
   65
   66      return undefined;
   67    },
   68  };

进入本章 →packages/core/src/core/client.ts:79–111 · 8 条证据
01
DIMENSION · ARCHITECTURE

架构与所有权

3 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factarchitecture

01 · 主 Harness 用递归 sendMessageStream 驱动多 turn，硬上限为 100

源码事实

GeminiClient 将单次 turn 交给 processTurn；若 next-speaker 判定模型应继续，或 AfterAgent hook 返回继续原因，就递归调用 sendMessageStream 并递减 boundedTurns，入口始终 clamp 到 MAX_TURNS=100。

白话解释

一次用户请求可以连续让模型说、用工具、再说；但最多转 100 圈，避免无尽自言自语。

对自研 Harness 的含义

控制流直观，递归路径共享 prompt_id 和 hook state，需要严格做 activeCalls 记账。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 79–94
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 95–111
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · bef6119500b0 · 打开账本提交 ↗

   79  const MAX_TURNS = 100;
   80
   81  type BeforeAgentHookReturn =
   82    | {
   83        type: GeminiEventType.AgentExecutionStopped;
   84        value: { reason: string; systemMessage?: string };
   85      }
   86    | {
   87        type: GeminiEventType.AgentExecutionBlocked;
   88        value: { reason: string; systemMessage?: string };
   89      }
   90    | { additionalContext: string | undefined }

   92
   93  export class GeminiClient {
   94    private chat?: GeminiChat;
   95    private sessionTurnCount = 0;
   96
   97    private readonly loopDetector: LoopDetectionService;
   98    private readonly compressionService: ChatCompressionService;
   99    private readonly agentHistoryProvider: AgentHistoryProvider;
  100    private readonly toolOutputMaskingService: ToolOutputMaskingService;
  101    private contextManager?: ContextManager;
  102    private lastPromptId: string;
  103    private currentSequenceModel: string | null = null;
  104    private lastSentIdeContext: IdeContext | undefined;
  105    private forceFullIdeContext = true;
  106
  107    /**
  108     * At any point in this conversation, was compression triggered without
  109     * being forced and did it fail?
  110     */
  111    private hasFailedCompressionAttempt = false;
  112
  113    constructor(private readonly context: AgentLoopContext) {
  114      this.loopDetector = new LoopDetectionService(this.config);
  115      this.compressionService = new ChatCompressionService();
  116      this.agentHistoryProvider = new AgentHistoryProvider(
  117        this.config.agentHistoryProviderConfig,
  118        this.config,
  119      );
  120      this.toolOutputMaskingService = new ToolOutputMaskingService();
  121      this.lastPromptId = this.config.getSessionId();
  122
  123      coreEvents.on(CoreEvent.ModelChanged, this.handleModelChanged);
  124      coreEvents.on(CoreEvent.MemoryChanged, this.handleMemoryChanged);
  125      coreEvents.on(
  126        CoreEvent.ApprovalModeChanged,
  127        this.handleApprovalModeChanged,
  128      );
  129    }
  130
  131    private get config(): Config {
  132      return this.context.config;
  133    }
  134
  135    private handleModelChanged = () => {
  136      this.currentSequenceModel = null;
  137    };
  138
  139    private handleMemoryChanged = () => {
  140      this.updateSystemInstruction();
  141    };
  142
  143    private handleApprovalModeChanged = (payload: ApprovalModeChangedPayload) => {
  144      if (payload.sessionId === this.config.getSessionId()) {
  145        this.updateSystemInstruction();
  146      }
  147    };
  148
  149    clearCurrentSequenceModel(): void {
  150      this.currentSequenceModel = null;
  151    }
  152
  153    // Hook state to deduplicate BeforeAgent calls and track response for
  154    // AfterAgent
  155    private hookStateMap = new Map<
  156      string,
  157      {
  158        hasFiredBeforeAgent: boolean;
  159        cumulativeResponse: string;
  160        activeCalls: number;
  161        originalRequest: PartListUnion;
  162      }
  163    >();
  164
  165    private async fireBeforeAgentHookSafe(
  166      request: PartListUnion,
  167      prompt_id: string,
  168    ): Promise<BeforeAgentHookReturn> {
  169      let hookState = this.hookStateMap.get(prompt_id);
  170      if (!hookState) {
  171        hookState = {
  172          hasFiredBeforeAgent: false,
  173          cumulativeResponse: '',
  174          activeCalls: 0,
  175          originalRequest: request,
  176        };
  177        this.hookStateMap.set(prompt_id, hookState);
  178      }
  179
  180      // Increment active calls for this prompt_id
  181      // This is called at the start of sendMessageStream, so it acts as an entry
  182      // counter. We increment here, assuming this helper is ALWAYS called at

02
L1factarchitecture

02 · 每轮先做上下文、溢出、IDE 配对和 loop 检测，再锁定模型与工具

源码事实

processTurn 先运行 context management/压缩与工具输出 masking，估算请求 token；为保持 functionCall→functionResponse 紧邻，会延迟 IDE context；随后 loop detector、model router/availability 和 model-dependent tool declarations 才进入 Turn.run。

白话解释

开口前先整理历史、确认装得下、保证工具回执不被编辑器消息插队，然后才选本轮模型和工具箱。

对自研 Harness 的含义

上下文与模型选择顺序清楚，工具描述可随模型变化且同一 sequence 保持模型粘性。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 614–663
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 664–715
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  614    private async *processTurn(
  615      request: PartListUnion,
  616      signal: AbortSignal,
  617      prompt_id: string,
  618      boundedTurns: number,
  619      displayContent?: PartListUnion,
  620    ): AsyncGenerator<ServerGeminiStreamEvent, Turn> {
  621      // Re-initialize turn (it was empty before if in loop, or new instance)
  622      let turn = new Turn(this.getChat(), prompt_id);
  623
  624      this.sessionTurnCount++;
  625      if (
  626        this.config.getMaxSessionTurns() > 0 &&
  627        this.sessionTurnCount > this.config.getMaxSessionTurns()
  628      ) {
  629        yield { type: GeminiEventType.MaxSessionTurns };
  630        return turn;
  631      }
  632
  633      if (!boundedTurns) {
  634        return turn;
  635      }
  636
  637      // Check for context window overflow
  638      const modelForLimitCheck = this._getActiveModelForCurrentTurn();
  639
  640      let currentBaseUnits = 0;
  641      let apiHistoryOverride: Content[] | undefined = undefined;
  642
  643      if (this.config.getContextManagementConfig().enabled) {
  644        if (this.contextManager) {
  645          const rawPendingRequest = createUserContent(request);
  646          const pendingRequest = {
  647            id: randomUUID(),
  648            content: rawPendingRequest,
  649          };
  650          const {
  651            history: newHistory,
  652            apiHistory,
  653            pendingApiHistory,
  654            baseUnits,
  655          } = await this.contextManager.renderHistory(
  656            pendingRequest,
  657            undefined,
  658            signal,
  659          );
  660
  661          currentBaseUnits = baseUnits;
  662
  663          // Use the PROCESSED pending content if available (e.g. if cleaned or distilled)
  664          const finalPendingContent =
  665            pendingApiHistory.length > 0
  666              ? pendingApiHistory[0]
  667              : rawPendingRequest;
  668
  669          // Late-bind the prompt: Append the active request to the managed history
  670          // only for the purpose of the upcoming API call.
  671          apiHistoryOverride = [...apiHistory, finalPendingContent];
  672
  673          this.getChat().setHistory(newHistory);
  674
  675          // Use the original request for display/recording,
  676          // but the processed one for the API and durable history.
  677          displayContent = rawPendingRequest.parts || [];
  678          request = finalPendingContent.parts || [];
  679        } else {
  680          const newHistory = await this.agentHistoryProvider.manageHistory(
  681            this.getHistory(),
  682            signal,
  683          );
  684          if (newHistory.length !== this.getHistory().length) {
  685            this.getChat().setHistory(newHistory);
  686          }
  687        }
  688      } else {
  689        const compressed = await this.tryCompressChat(prompt_id, false, signal);
  690
  691        if (compressed.compressionStatus === CompressionStatus.COMPRESSED) {
  692          yield { type: GeminiEventType.ChatCompressed, value: compressed };
  693        }
  694      }
  695
  696      const remainingTokenCount =
  697        tokenLimit(modelForLimitCheck) - this.getChat().getLastPromptTokenCount();
  698
  699      await this.tryMaskToolOutputs(this.getHistory());
  700
  701      // Estimate tokens. For text-only requests, we estimate based on character length.
  702      // For requests with non-text parts (like images, tools), we use the countTokens API.
  703      const estimatedRequestTokenCount = await calculateRequestTokenCount(
  704        request,
  705        this.getContentGeneratorOrFail(),
  706        modelForLimitCheck,
  707      );
  708
  709      if (estimatedRequestTokenCount > remainingTokenCount) {
  710        yield {
  711          type: GeminiEventType.ContextWindowWillOverflow,
  712          value: { estimatedRequestTokenCount, remainingTokenCount },
  713        };
  714        return turn;
  715      }
  716
  717      // Prevent context updates from being sent while a tool call is
  718      // waiting for a response. The Gemini API requires that a functionResponse
  719      // part from the user immediately follows a functionCall part from the model
  720      // in the conversation history . The IDE context is not discarded; it will
  721      // be included in the next regular message sent to the model.
  722      const history = this.getHistory();
  723      const lastMessage =
  724        history.length > 0 ? history[history.length - 1] : undefined;
  725      const hasPendingToolCall =
  726        !!lastMessage &&
  727        lastMessage.role === 'model' &&
  728        (lastMessage.parts?.some((p) => 'functionCall' in p) || false);
  729
  730      if (this.config.getIdeMode() && !hasPendingToolCall) {
  731        const { contextParts, newIdeContext } = this.getIdeContextParts(
  732          this.forceFullIdeContext || history.length === 0,
  733        );
  734        if (contextParts.length > 0) {
  735          this.getChat().addHistory({
  736            role: 'user',
  737            parts: [{ text: contextParts.join('\n') }],
  738          });
  739        }
  740        this.lastSentIdeContext = newIdeContext;
  741        this.forceFullIdeContext = false;
  742      }
  743
  744      // Re-initialize turn with fresh history
  745      turn = new Turn(this.getChat(), prompt_id);
  746
  747      const loopResult = await this.loopDetector.turnStarted(signal);
  748      if (loopResult.count > 1) {
  749        yield { type: GeminiEventType.LoopDetected };
  750        return turn;
  751      } else if (loopResult.count === 1) {
  752        if (boundedTurns <= 1) {
  753          yield { type: GeminiEventType.MaxSessionTurns };
  754          return turn;
  755        }
  756        return yield* this._recoverFromLoop(
  757          loopResult,
  758          signal,
  759          prompt_id,
  760          boundedTurns,
  761          displayContent,
  762        );
  763      }
  764
  765      const routingContext: RoutingContext = {
  766        history: this.getChat().getHistory(/*curated=*/ true),
  767        request,
  768        signal,
  769        requestedModel: this.config.getModel(),
  770      };
  771
  772      let modelToUse: string;
  773
  774      // Determine Model (Stickiness vs. Routing)
  775      if (this.currentSequenceModel) {
  776        modelToUse = this.currentSequenceModel;
  777      } else {
  778        const router = this.config.getModelRouterService();
  779        const decision = await router.route(routingContext);
  780        modelToUse = decision.model;
  781      }
  782
  783      // availability logic
  784      const modelConfigKey: ModelConfigKey = {
  785        model: modelToUse,
  786        isChatModel: true,

03
L1factarchitecture

03 · 循环检测能先恢复一次，再判定硬循环

源码事实

turnStarted 和每个流事件都送入 LoopDetectionService；count=1 进入 _recoverFromLoop，count>1 发 LoopDetected 并终止，剩余 turn 不足时转 MaxSessionTurns。

白话解释

第一次怀疑绕圈会给模型一次纠偏机会，第二次还绕就停。

对自研 Harness 的含义

比仅靠 turn 上限更早止损，并保留一次自恢复空间。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 744–752
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 753–763
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

03
DIMENSION · MODELCONTEXT

模型与上下文

8 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L2factmodelContext

01 · 统一 ContentGenerator 契约覆盖流式、非流式、计数与 embedding

源码事实

ContentGenerator 抽象 generateContent、generateContentStream、countTokens 和 embedContent；AuthType 覆盖 Google OAuth、Gemini API key、Vertex AI、ADC 与 Gateway。

白话解释

上层只认一套生成接口，底下可换个人 Google 登录、API key、企业 Vertex 或网关。

对自研 Harness 的含义

Provider 切换不改主循环，但 thought signature 兼容性仍需在切换认证后处理。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 35–51

    分支、副作用和回执

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   64    LOGIN_WITH_GOOGLE = 'oauth-personal',
   65    USE_GEMINI = 'gemini-api-key',
   66    USE_VERTEX_AI = 'vertex-ai',
   67    LEGACY_CLOUD_SHELL = 'cloud-shell',
   68    COMPUTE_ADC = 'compute-default-credentials',
   69    GATEWAY = 'gateway',
   70  }
   71
   72  /**
   73   * Detects the best authentication type based on environment variables.
   74   *
   75   * Checks in order:
   76   * 1. GOOGLE_GENAI_USE_GCA=true -> LOGIN_WITH_GOOGLE
   77   * 2. GOOGLE_GENAI_USE_VERTEXAI=true -> USE_VERTEX_AI
   78   * 3. GEMINI_API_KEY -> USE_GEMINI
   79   */
   80  export function getAuthTypeFromEnv(): AuthType | undefined {
   81    if (process.env['GOOGLE_GENAI_USE_GCA'] === 'true') {
   82      return AuthType.LOGIN_WITH_GOOGLE;
   83    }
   84    if (process.env['GOOGLE_GENAI_USE_VERTEXAI'] === 'true') {
   85      return AuthType.USE_VERTEX_AI;
   86    }
   87    if (process.env['GOOGLE_GEMINI_BASE_URL']) {
   88      return AuthType.GATEWAY;
   89    }
   90    if (process.env['GEMINI_API_KEY']) {
   91      return AuthType.USE_GEMINI;
   92    }
   93    if (
   94      process.env['CLOUD_SHELL'] === 'true' ||
   95      process.env['GEMINI_CLI_USE_COMPUTE_ADC'] === 'true'
   96    ) {
   97      return AuthType.COMPUTE_ADC;
   98    }
   99    return undefined;
  100  }
  101

  103    apiKey?: string;
  104    vertexai?: boolean;
  105    authType?: AuthType;
  106    proxy?: string;
  107    baseUrl?: string;
  108    customHeaders?: Record<string, string>;
  109    vertexAiRouting?: VertexAiRoutingConfig;
  110  };

  113  export type VertexAiSharedRequestType = 'priority' | 'flex';

  116    requestType?: VertexAiRequestType;

  122    'X-Vertex-AI-LLM-Shared-Request-Type';

  125   * Vertex AI Representative Endpoints (REP) for US and EU multi-regions.
  126   * These are used as a workaround for the client dynamically
  127   * constructing default legacy hostnames (e.g., 'us-aiplatform.googleapis.com')
  128   * instead of routing to the official REP endpoints.

  130  const VERTEX_AI_US_REP_ENDPOINT = 'https://aiplatform.us.rep.googleapis.com';
  131  const VERTEX_AI_EU_REP_ENDPOINT = 'https://aiplatform.eu.rep.googleapis.com';

  134    try {
  135      new URL(baseUrl);
  136    } catch {
  137      throw new Error(`Invalid custom base URL: ${baseUrl}`);

02
L1factmodelContext

02 · 个人/ADC 走 Code Assist，API key/Vertex/Gateway 走 Google GenAI SDK

源码事实

createContentGenerator 对 LOGIN_WITH_GOOGLE/COMPUTE_ADC 创建 CodeAssistServer 并加模型映射；Gemini/Vertex/Gateway 构造 GoogleGenAI，支持自定义 base URL、headers、proxy 和 Vertex routing headers。

白话解释

登录方式不仅换凭证，也可能换后端客户端；企业 Vertex 还能指定共享/专用路由。

对自研 Harness 的含义

同一主循环有多条传输实现，认证切换测试必须覆盖历史 thoughtSignature 清理。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 285–296
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 297–310
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

03
L1factmodelContext

03 · 连接阶段与中途流错误分开重试，中途流最多四次尝试

源码事实

GeminiChat 的 streamWithRetries 把连接阶段错误交给底层 retryWithBackoff；流迭代中的可重试网络/内容错误使用独立指数退避，并限制到 MID_STREAM_RETRY_OPTIONS 的四次总尝试。

白话解释

连不上和连上后半路断掉是两类事故，分别计数；不会因为全局重试设得很大就反复重播半截响应。

对自研 Harness 的含义

降低 mid-stream 重复输出/工具调用风险。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 517–546
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 547–578
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · bef6119500b0 · 打开账本提交 ↗
packages/core/src/core/geminiChat.ts:517–578固定提交 ↗
  517      const streamWithRetries = async function* (
  518        this: GeminiChat,
  519      ): AsyncGenerator<StreamEvent, void, void> {
  520        try {
  521          const maxAttempts = this.context.config.getMaxAttempts();
  522
  523          for (let attempt = 0; attempt < maxAttempts; attempt++) {
  524            let isConnectionPhase = true;
  525            try {
  526              if (attempt > 0) {
  527                yield { type: StreamEventType.RETRY };
  528              }
  529
  530              // If this is a retry, update the key with the new context.
  531              const currentConfigKey =
  532                attempt > 0
  533                  ? { ...modelConfigKey, isRetry: true }
  534                  : modelConfigKey;
  535
  536              isConnectionPhase = true;
  537              const stream = await this.makeApiCallAndProcessStream(
  538                currentConfigKey,
  539                requestHistory,
  540                prompt_id,
  541                signal,
  542                role,
  543                apiHistoryOverride,
  544              );
  545              isConnectionPhase = false;
  546              for await (const chunk of stream) {
  547                yield { type: StreamEventType.CHUNK, value: chunk };
  548              }
  549
  550              return;
  551            } catch (error) {
  552              if (error instanceof AgentExecutionStoppedError) {
  553                yield {
  554                  type: StreamEventType.AGENT_EXECUTION_STOPPED,
  555                  reason: error.reason,
  556                };
  557                return; // Stop the generator
  558              }
  559
  560              if (error instanceof AgentExecutionBlockedError) {
  561                yield {
  562                  type: StreamEventType.AGENT_EXECUTION_BLOCKED,
  563                  reason: error.reason,
  564                };
  565                if (error.syntheticResponse) {
  566                  yield {
  567                    type: StreamEventType.CHUNK,
  568                    value: error.syntheticResponse,
  569                  };
  570                }
  571                return; // Stop the generator
  572              }
  573
  574              if (isConnectionPhase) {
  575                // Connection phase errors have already been retried by retryWithBackoff.
  576                // If they bubble up here, they are exhausted or fatal.
  577                throw error;
  578              }
  579
  580              // Check if the error is retryable (e.g., transient SSL errors
  581              // like ERR_SSL_SSLV3_ALERT_BAD_RECORD_MAC or ApiError)
  582              const isRetryable = isRetryableError(
  583                error,
  584                this.context.config.getRetryFetchErrors(),
  585              );
  586
  587              const isContentError = error instanceof InvalidStreamError;
  588              const isRetryableContentError =
  589                isContentError && error.type !== 'NO_RESPONSE_TEXT';
  590              const errorType = isContentError
  591                ? error.type
  592                : getRetryErrorType(error);
  593
  594              if (isRetryableContentError || (isRetryable && !signal.aborted)) {
  595                // The issue requests exactly 3 retries (4 attempts) for API errors during stream iteration.
  596                // Regardless of the global maxAttempts (e.g. 10), we only want to retry these mid-stream API errors
  597                // up to 3 times before finally throwing the error to the user.
  598                const maxMidStreamAttempts = MID_STREAM_RETRY_OPTIONS.maxAttempts;
  599
  600                if (
  601                  attempt < maxAttempts - 1 &&
  602                  attempt < maxMidStreamAttempts - 1
  603                ) {
  604                  const delayMs = MID_STREAM_RETRY_OPTIONS.useExponentialBackoff
  605                    ? MID_STREAM_RETRY_OPTIONS.initialDelayMs *
  606                      Math.pow(2, attempt)
  607                    : MID_STREAM_RETRY_OPTIONS.initialDelayMs * (attempt + 1);
  608
  609                  if (isContentError) {
  610                    logContentRetry(
  611                      this.context.config,
  612                      new ContentRetryEvent(attempt, errorType, delayMs, model),
  613                    );
  614                  } else {
  615                    logNetworkRetryAttempt(
  616                      this.context.config,
  617                      new NetworkRetryAttemptEvent(
  618                        attempt + 1,
  619                        maxAttempts,
  620                        errorType,
  621                        delayMs,
  622                        model,
  623                      ),
  624                    );
  625                  }
  626                  coreEvents.emitRetryAttempt({
  627                    attempt: attempt + 1,
  628                    maxAttempts: Math.min(maxAttempts, maxMidStreamAttempts),
  629                    delayMs,
  630                    error: errorType,
  631                    model,
  632                  });
  633                  await new Promise((res) => setTimeout(res, delayMs));
  634                  continue;
  635                }
  636              }
  637
  638              // If we've aborted, we throw without logging a failure.
  639              if (signal.aborted) {
  640                throw error;
  641              }
  642
  643              logContentRetryFailure(
  644                this.context.config,
  645                new ContentRetryFailureEvent(attempt + 1, errorType, model),
  646              );
  647
  648              throw error;
  649            }

  548      const streamWithRetries = async function* (
  549        this: GeminiChat,
  550      ): AsyncGenerator<StreamEvent, void, void> {
  551        try {
  552          const maxAttempts = this.context.config.getMaxAttempts();
  553          let lastStreamError: unknown = undefined;
  554
  555          for (let attempt = 0; attempt < maxAttempts; attempt++) {
  556            let isConnectionPhase = true;
  557            try {
  558              if (attempt > 0) {
  559                yield { type: StreamEventType.RETRY };
  560              }
  561
  562              // If this is a retry, update the key with the new context.
  563              const currentConfigKey =
  564                attempt > 0
  565                  ? { ...modelConfigKey, isRetry: true, lastStreamError }
  566                  : modelConfigKey;
  567
  568              isConnectionPhase = true;
  569              const stream = await this.makeApiCallAndProcessStream(
  570                currentConfigKey,
  571                requestHistory,
  572                prompt_id,
  573                signal,
  574                role,
  575                apiHistoryOverride,
  576              );
  577              isConnectionPhase = false;
  578              for await (const chunk of stream) {
  579                yield { type: StreamEventType.CHUNK, value: chunk };
  580              }
  581
  582              return;
  583            } catch (error) {
  584              if (error instanceof InvalidStreamError) {
  585                lastStreamError = error;
  586              }
  587
  588              if (error instanceof AgentExecutionStoppedError) {
  589                yield {
  590                  type: StreamEventType.AGENT_EXECUTION_STOPPED,
  591                  reason: error.reason,
  592                };
  593                return; // Stop the generator
  594              }
  595
  596              if (error instanceof AgentExecutionBlockedError) {
  597                yield {
  598                  type: StreamEventType.AGENT_EXECUTION_BLOCKED,
  599                  reason: error.reason,
  600                };
  601                if (error.syntheticResponse) {
  602                  yield {
  603                    type: StreamEventType.CHUNK,
  604                    value: error.syntheticResponse,
  605                  };
  606                }
  607                return; // Stop the generator
  608              }
  609
  610              if (isConnectionPhase) {
  611                // Connection phase errors have already been retried by retryWithBackoff.
  612                // If they bubble up here, they are exhausted or fatal.
  613                throw error;
  614              }
  615
  616              // Check if the error is retryable (e.g., transient SSL errors
  617              // like ERR_SSL_SSLV3_ALERT_BAD_RECORD_MAC or ApiError)
  618              const isRetryable = isRetryableError(
  619                error,
  620                this.context.config.getRetryFetchErrors(),
  621              );
  622
  623              const isContentError = error instanceof InvalidStreamError;
  624              const isRetryableContentError = isContentError;
  625              const errorType = isContentError
  626                ? error.type
  627                : getRetryErrorType(error);
  628
  629              if (isRetryableContentError || (isRetryable && !signal.aborted)) {
  630                // The issue requests exactly 3 retries (4 attempts) for API errors during stream iteration.
  631                // Regardless of the global maxAttempts (e.g. 10), we only want to retry these mid-stream API errors
  632                // up to 3 times before finally throwing the error to the user.
  633                const maxMidStreamAttempts = MID_STREAM_RETRY_OPTIONS.maxAttempts;
  634
  635                if (
  636                  attempt < maxAttempts - 1 &&
  637                  attempt < maxMidStreamAttempts - 1
  638                ) {
  639                  const delayMs = MID_STREAM_RETRY_OPTIONS.useExponentialBackoff
  640                    ? MID_STREAM_RETRY_OPTIONS.initialDelayMs *
  641                      Math.pow(2, attempt)
  642                    : MID_STREAM_RETRY_OPTIONS.initialDelayMs * (attempt + 1);
  643
  644                  if (isContentError) {
  645                    logContentRetry(
  646                      this.context.config,
  647                      new ContentRetryEvent(attempt, errorType, delayMs, model),
  648                    );
  649                  } else {
04
L1factmodelContext

04 · Legacy 压缩默认在 50% 窗口触发，并保留最近约 30%

源码事实

ChatCompressionService 默认阈值为模型 token limit 的 0.5，压缩切点按序列化字符量寻找安全 user turn，目标保留最近 30%，且不会拆断 function call/response 边界。

白话解释

箱子装到一半就提前整理，最近三成原文留下，旧七成写成摘要；切口只选完整对话边界。

对自研 Harness 的含义

为长回复和工具调用预留较大余量，代价是较早产生摘要成本。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 37–43
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 44–52
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

packages/core/src/context/chatCompressionService.ts:37–52固定提交 ↗
   37  /**
   38   * Default threshold for compression token count as a fraction of the model's
   39   * token limit. If the chat history exceeds this threshold, it will be compressed.
   40   */
   41  const DEFAULT_COMPRESSION_TOKEN_THRESHOLD = 0.5;
   42
   43  /**
   44   * The fraction of the latest chat history to keep. A value of 0.3
   45   * means that only the last 30% of the chat history will be kept after compression.
   46   */
   47  const COMPRESSION_PRESERVE_THRESHOLD = 0.3;
   48
   49  /**
   50   * The budget for function response tokens in the preserved history.
   51   */
   52  const COMPRESSION_FUNCTION_RESPONSE_TOKEN_BUDGET = 50_000;
   53
   54  /**
   55   * Returns the index of the oldest item to keep when compressing. May return
   56   * contents.length which indicates that everything should be compressed.
   57   *
   58   * Exported for testing purposes.
   59   */
   60  export function findCompressSplitPoint(
   61    contents: Content[],
   62    fraction: number,
   63  ): number {
   64    if (fraction <= 0 || fraction >= 1) {
   65      throw new Error('Fraction must be between 0 and 1');
   66    }
   67
   68    const charCounts = contents.map((content) => JSON.stringify(content).length);
   69    const totalCharCount = charCounts.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
   70    const targetCharCount = totalCharCount * fraction;
   71
   72    let lastSplitPoint = 0; // 0 is always valid (compress nothing)
   73    let cumulativeCharCount = 0;
   74    for (let i = 0; i < contents.length; i++) {
   75      const content = contents[i];
   76      if (
   77        content.role === 'user' &&
   78        !content.parts?.some((part) => !!part.functionResponse)
   79      ) {
   80        if (cumulativeCharCount >= targetCharCount) {
   81          return i;
   82        }
   83        lastSplitPoint = i;
   84      }
   85      cumulativeCharCount += charCounts[i];
   86    }
   87
   88    // We found no split points after targetCharCount.
   89    // Check if it's safe to compress everything.
   90    const lastContent = contents[contents.length - 1];
   91    if (
   92      lastContent?.role === 'model' &&
   93      !lastContent?.parts?.some((part) => part.functionCall)
   94    ) {
   95      return contents.length;
   96    }
   97
   98    // Can't compress everything so just compress at last splitpoint.
   99    return lastSplitPoint;
  100  }
  101
  102  export function modelStringToModelConfigAlias(model: string): string {
  103    switch (model) {
  104      case PREVIEW_GEMINI_MODEL:
  105      case PREVIEW_GEMINI_3_1_MODEL:
  106        return 'chat-compression-3-pro';
  107      case PREVIEW_GEMINI_FLASH_MODEL:
  108        return 'chat-compression-3-flash';
  109      case PREVIEW_GEMINI_FLASH_LITE_MODEL:
  110      // fallthrough
  111      case DEFAULT_GEMINI_FLASH_LITE_MODEL:
  112        return 'chat-compression-3.1-flash-lite';
  113      case 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite':
  114        return 'chat-compression-2.5-flash-lite';
  115      case DEFAULT_GEMINI_MODEL:
  116        return 'chat-compression-2.5-pro';
  117      case DEFAULT_GEMINI_FLASH_MODEL:
  118        return 'chat-compression-2.5-flash';
  119      default:
  120        return 'chat-compression-default';
  121    }
  122  }

L1factmodelContext

05 · 旧工具输出采用反向预算，超额内容落临时文件并只留尾部

源码事实

压缩前从最新向最旧累计 function response token，优先保留近端；超过 50k 工具响应预算后，将较老大输出保存到文件并替换为最后 30 行和路径提示。

白话解释

最新日志全文留在桌面，旧日志搬进档案室，只在上下文里留末尾和取件地址。

对自研 Harness 的含义

既省 token 又保留可恢复原文，比不可逆截断更适合调试。

逐段阅读提示

    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

    分支、副作用和回执

叙述来自历史证据账本 · bef6119500b0 · 打开账本提交 ↗
packages/core/src/context/chatCompressionService.ts:124–142固定提交 ↗
  124  /**
  125   * Processes the chat history to ensure function responses don't exceed a specific token budget.
  126   *
  127   * This function implements a "Reverse Token Budget" strategy:
  128   * 1. It iterates through the history from the most recent turn to the oldest.
  129   * 2. It keeps a running tally of tokens used by function responses.
  130   * 3. Recent tool outputs are preserved in full to maintain high-fidelity context for the current turn.
  131   * 4. Once the budget (COMPRESSION_FUNCTION_RESPONSE_TOKEN_BUDGET) is exceeded, any older large
  132   *    tool responses are truncated to their last 30 lines and saved to a temporary file.
  133   *
  134   * This ensures that compression effectively reduces context size even when recent turns
  135   * contain massive tool outputs (like large grep results or logs).
  136   */
  137  async function truncateHistoryToBudget(
  138    history: readonly Content[],
  139    config: Config,
  140  ): Promise<Content[]> {
  141    let functionResponseTokenCounter = 0;
  142    const truncatedHistory: Content[] = [];
  143
  144    // Iterate backwards: newest messages first to prioritize their context.
  145    for (let i = history.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
  146      const content = history[i];
  147      const newParts = [];
  148
  149      if (content.parts) {
  150        // Process parts of the message backwards as well.
  151        for (let j = content.parts.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
  152          const part = content.parts[j];
  153
  154          if (part.functionResponse) {
  155            const responseObj = part.functionResponse.response;
  156            // Ensure we have a string representation to truncate.
  157            // If the response is an object, we try to extract a primary string field (output or content).
  158            let contentStr: string;
  159            if (typeof responseObj === 'string') {
  160              contentStr = responseObj;
  161            } else if (responseObj && typeof responseObj === 'object') {
  162              if (
  163                'output' in responseObj &&
  164                // eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-syntax
  165                typeof responseObj['output'] === 'string'
  166              ) {
  167                contentStr = responseObj['output'];
  168              } else if (
  169                'content' in responseObj &&
  170                // eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-syntax
  171                typeof responseObj['content'] === 'string'
  172              ) {
  173                contentStr = responseObj['content'];
  174              } else {
  175                contentStr = JSON.stringify(responseObj, null, 2);
  176              }
  177            } else {
  178              contentStr = JSON.stringify(responseObj, null, 2);
  179            }
  180
  181            const tokens = estimateTokenCountSync([{ text: contentStr }]);
  182
  183            if (
  184              functionResponseTokenCounter + tokens >
  185              COMPRESSION_FUNCTION_RESPONSE_TOKEN_BUDGET
  186            ) {
  187              try {
  188                // Budget exceeded: Truncate this response.
  189                const { outputFile } = await saveTruncatedToolOutput(
  190                  contentStr,
  191                  part.functionResponse.name ?? 'unknown_tool',
  192                  config.getNextCompressionTruncationId(),
  193                  config.storage.getProjectTempDir(),
  194                );
  195
  196                const truncatedMessage = formatTruncatedToolOutput(
  197                  contentStr,
  198                  outputFile,
  199                  config.getTruncateToolOutputThreshold(),
  200                );
  201
  202                newParts.unshift({
  203                  functionResponse: {
  204                    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-misused-spread
  205                    ...part.functionResponse,
  206                    response: { output: truncatedMessage },
  207                  },
  208                });
  209
  210                // Count the small truncated placeholder towards the budget.
  211                functionResponseTokenCounter += estimateTokenCountSync([
  212                  { text: truncatedMessage },
  213                ]);

06 · 摘要膨胀会触发熔断，随后只做内容截断

源码事实

tryCompressChat 记录 COMPRESSION_FAILED_INFLATED_TOKEN_COUNT；非强制压缩一旦失败即保持 hasFailedCompressionAttempt，后续服务可返回 CONTENT_TRUNCATED 并直接更新 history，不重建 chat。

白话解释

如果摘要反而比原文胖，就不再每轮花钱重写摘要，改用更轻的裁剪。

对自研 Harness 的含义

避免压缩风暴，但本会话之后的语义保真会更多依赖工具输出 masking。
