07
L1factmodelContext

07 · 新 ContextManager 是图与流水线系统，带 preview late-bind、压力屏障、GC/蒸馏和结构校验

源码事实

ContextManager 从 durable AgentChatHistory 同步 pristine graph，对 pending request 建 ephemeral preview，等待 pipeline/hot-start barrier，执行 triggers，再带保护节点渲染、harden 与 invariant check；相同 node hash 可复用 render cache。

白话解释

新系统不再把历史当一长串消息，而是当可追溯的节点图；当前问题先在草稿区处理，确认后才影响长期账本。

对自研 Harness 的含义

能做精细蒸馏和增量管理，但复杂度显著高于 legacy summary，必须明确 feature gate 与回退路径。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 26–56
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 57–88
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · bef6119500b0 · 打开账本提交 ↗
packages/core/src/context/contextManager.ts:26–88固定提交 ↗
   26  export class ContextManager {
   27    // Master state containing the pristine graph and current active graph.
   28    private buffer: ContextWorkingBufferImpl =
   29      ContextWorkingBufferImpl.initialize([]);
   30
   31    private readonly eventBus: ContextEventBus;
   32    private readonly orchestrator: PipelineOrchestrator;
   33
   34    // Track what IDs have been evaluated for triggers to prevent redundant processing
   35    private readonly evaluatedNodeIds = new Set<string>();
   36
   37    // Hysteresis tracking to prevent utility call churn
   38    private lastTriggeredDeficit = 0;
   39    private lastTriggeredNormalizeDeficit = 0;
   40
   41    // Cache for Anomaly 3 (Redundant Renders)
   42    private lastRenderCache?: {
   43      nodesHash: string;
   44      result: {
   45        history: HistoryTurn[];
   46        apiHistory: Content[];
   47        pendingApiHistory: Content[];
   48        didApplyManagement: boolean;
   49        baseUnits: number;
   50        processedNodes: readonly ConcreteNode[];
   51      };
   52    };
   53
   54    private hasPerformedHotStart = false;
   55
   56    constructor(
   57      private readonly sidecar: ContextProfile,
   58      private readonly env: ContextEnvironment,
   59      private readonly tracer: ContextTracer,
   60      orchestrator: PipelineOrchestrator,
   61      private readonly chatHistory: AgentChatHistory,
   62      private readonly advancedTokenCalculator: AdvancedTokenCalculator,
   63      private readonly headerProvider?: () => Promise<Content | undefined>,
   64    ) {
   65      this.eventBus = env.eventBus;
   66      this.orchestrator = orchestrator;
   67
   68      // Direct synchronization: ContextManager is the "Pull Master"
   69      // and tells the orchestrator what to do.
   70      this.orchestrator.setNodeProvider(() => this.buffer.nodes);
   71
   72      this.eventBus.onProcessorResult((event) => {
   73        // Defensive: Verify all targets are still present in the buffer.
   74        const bufferIds = new Set(this.buffer.nodes.map((n) => n.id));
   75        if (!event.targets.every((t) => bufferIds.has(t.id))) {
   76          debugLogger.warn(
   77            `[ContextManager] Dropping processor result from ${event.processorId}: targets no longer in buffer.`,
   78          );
   79          return;
   80        }
   81
   82        this.buffer = this.buffer.applyProcessorResult(
   83          event.processorId,
   84          event.targets,
   85          event.returnedNodes,
   86        );
   87      });
   88    }
   89
   90    async renderHistory(
   91      pendingRequest?: HistoryTurn,
   92      activeTaskIds: Set<string> = new Set(),
   93      abortSignal?: AbortSignal,
   94    ): Promise<{
   95      history: HistoryTurn[];
   96      apiHistory: Content[];
   97      pendingApiHistory: Content[];
   98      didApplyManagement: boolean;
   99      baseUnits: number;
  100      processedNodes: readonly ConcreteNode[];
  101    }> {
  102      this.tracer.logEvent('ContextManager', 'Starting rendering of LLM context');
  103
  104      // 1. Explicit Sync with the durable history.
  105      const currentHistory = this.chatHistory.get();
  106      const pristineNodes = this.env.graphMapper.sync(currentHistory);
  107
  108      this.buffer = this.buffer.syncPristineHistory(pristineNodes);
  109
  110      // Identify truly "new" nodes that haven't been evaluated for triggers yet.
  111      const newPrimalNodes = new Set<string>();
  112      for (const node of pristineNodes) {
  113        if (!this.evaluatedNodeIds.has(node.id)) {
  114          newPrimalNodes.add(node.id);
  115          this.evaluatedNodeIds.add(node.id);
  116        }
  117      }
  118
  119      // 2. Preview the pending request.
  120      let previewNodes: readonly ConcreteNode[] = [];
  121      if (pendingRequest) {
  122        const syncedNodes = this.env.graphMapper.sync([pendingRequest]);
  123        const previewNodeIds = new Set(syncedNodes.map((n) => n.id));
  124
  125        const previewBuffer = ContextWorkingBufferImpl.initialize(syncedNodes);
  126
  127        const processedPreviewBuffer = await this.orchestrator.executeTriggerSync(
  128          'new_message',
  129          previewBuffer,
  130          previewNodeIds,
  131        );
  132
  133        previewNodes = processedPreviewBuffer.nodes;
  134      }
  135
  136      // --- Hot Start Calibration ---
  137      const hotStartPromise = (async () => {
  138        if (!this.hasPerformedHotStart) {
  139          this.hasPerformedHotStart = true;
  140          if (this.buffer.nodes.length > 0) {
  141            const nodesForHotStart = [...this.buffer.nodes, ...previewNodes];
  142            await this.performHotStartCalibration(nodesForHotStart, abortSignal);
  143          }
  144        }
  145      })();
  146
  147      // 3. Synchronous Pressure Barrier
  148      await Promise.all([this.orchestrator.waitForPipelines(), hotStartPromise]);
  149
  150      let nodes = this.buffer.nodes;
  151      const previewNodeIds = new Set<string>();
  152
  153      if (previewNodes.length > 0) {
  154        nodes = [...nodes, ...previewNodes];
  155        for (const node of previewNodes) {
  156          previewNodeIds.add(node.id);
  157        }
  158      }
  159

08
L1factmodelContext

08 · 新上下文系统用真实 API token 反校准本地估算

源码事实

processTurn 把 ContextManager 渲染得到的 baseUnits 带入请求；Finished 事件若包含 promptTokenCount，则通过 eventBus 发 actualTokens 与 promptBaseUnits ground truth。

白话解释

先用本地尺子估，再用模型 API 的过磅结果校准尺子。

对自研 Harness 的含义

长期预算判断可适应多模态与不同模型 tokenizer 偏差。

DIMENSION · TOOLS

工具注册与执行

2 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1facttools

01 · Turn 只解析模型流，工具执行交给独立 event-driven Scheduler

源码事实

Turn 从 generateContentStream 收集 text、thought、functionCall、usage 和 finish reason，将 functionCall 放进 pendingToolCalls；主/子 Agent 再以各自 registry、message bus、sandbox manager 创建 Scheduler 批量执行。

白话解释

模型流负责开任务单，调度器负责审批、排队、执行和回执；两者不是揉在一个 switch 里。

对自研 Harness 的含义

主 Agent 和子 Agent 能复用同一工具治理链，同时各自限定工具集。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 236–277
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 278–320
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · bef6119500b0 · 打开账本提交 ↗
packages/core/src/core/turn.ts:236–320固定提交 ↗
  236    | ServerGeminiModelInfoEvent
  237    | ServerGeminiAgentExecutionStoppedEvent
  238    | ServerGeminiAgentExecutionBlockedEvent;
  239
  240  // A turn manages the agentic loop turn within the server context.
  241  export class Turn {
  242    private callCounter = 0;
  243
  244    readonly pendingToolCalls: ToolCallRequestInfo[] = [];
  245    private debugResponses: GenerateContentResponse[] = [];
  246    private pendingCitations = new Set<string>();
  247    private cachedResponseText: string | undefined = undefined;
  248    finishReason: FinishReason | undefined = undefined;
  249    private hasLoggedRagTrace = false;
  250
  251    constructor(
  252      private readonly chat: GeminiChat,
  253      private readonly prompt_id: string,
  254    ) {}
  255
  256    // The run method yields simpler events suitable for server logic
  257    async *run(
  258      modelConfigKey: ModelConfigKey,
  259      req: PartListUnion,
  260      signal: AbortSignal,
  261      options: {
  262        displayContent?: PartListUnion;
  263        role?: LlmRole;
  264        apiHistoryOverride?: Content[];
  265      } = {},
  266    ): AsyncGenerator<ServerGeminiStreamEvent> {
  267      const { displayContent, role = LlmRole.MAIN, apiHistoryOverride } = options;
  268      try {
  269        // Note: This assumes `sendMessageStream` yields events like
  270        // { type: StreamEventType.RETRY } or { type: StreamEventType.CHUNK, value: GenerateContentResponse }
  271        const responseStream = await this.chat.sendMessageStream(
  272          modelConfigKey,
  273          req,
  274          this.prompt_id,
  275          signal,
  276          role,
  277          displayContent,
  278          apiHistoryOverride,
  279        );
  280
  281        for await (const streamEvent of responseStream) {
  282          if (signal?.aborted) {
  283            yield { type: GeminiEventType.UserCancelled };
  284            return;
  285          }
  286
  287          // Handle the new RETRY event
  288          if (streamEvent.type === 'retry') {
  289            yield { type: GeminiEventType.Retry };
  290            continue; // Skip to the next event in the stream
  291          }
  292
  293          if (streamEvent.type === 'agent_execution_stopped') {
  294            yield {
  295              type: GeminiEventType.AgentExecutionStopped,
  296              value: { reason: streamEvent.reason },
  297            };
  298            return;
  299          }
  300
  301          if (streamEvent.type === 'agent_execution_blocked') {
  302            yield {
  303              type: GeminiEventType.AgentExecutionBlocked,
  304              value: { reason: streamEvent.reason },
  305            };
  306            continue;
  307          }
  308
  309          // Assuming other events are chunks with a `value` property
  310          const resp = streamEvent.value;
  311          if (!resp) continue; // Skip if there's no response body
  312
  313          // Log RAG trace if enabled (only once per turn to avoid log bloat on streams)
  314          if (
  315            !this.hasLoggedRagTrace &&
  316            this.chat.context.config.getLogRagSnippets?.()
  317          ) {
  318            let ragStatus: string | undefined;
  319            let snippets: RagSnippet[] | undefined;
  320
  321            if (
  322              typeof resp === 'object' &&
  323              resp !== null &&
  324              'metadata' in resp &&
  325              typeof resp.metadata === 'object' &&
  326              resp.metadata !== null
  327            ) {
  328              const metadata = resp.metadata as {
  329                ragStatus?: string;
  330                snippets?: RagSnippet[];
  331              };
  332              ragStatus = metadata.ragStatus;
  333              snippets = metadata.snippets;
  334            }
  335
  336            if (ragStatus || snippets) {
  337              ragLogger.log({
  338                sessionId: this.chat.context.config.getSessionId(),
  339                ragStatus: ragStatus ?? 'UNKNOWN',
  340                snippets: snippets ?? [],
  341              });
  342              this.hasLoggedRagTrace = true;
  343            }
  344          }
  345
  346          this.debugResponses.push(resp);
  347
  348          const traceId = resp.responseId;
  349
  350          const parts = resp.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts ?? [];
  351          for (const part of parts) {
  352            if (part.thought) {
  353              const thought = parseThought(part.text ?? '');
  354              yield {
  355                type: GeminiEventType.Thought,
  356                value: thought,
  357                traceId,
  358              };
  359            }
  360          }
  361
  362          const text = getResponseText(resp);
  363          if (text) {
  364            yield { type: GeminiEventType.Content, value: text, traceId };
  365          }
  366
  367          // Handle function calls (requesting tool execution)
  368          const functionCalls = resp.functionCalls ?? [];
  369          for (const fnCall of functionCalls) {
  370            const event = this.handlePendingFunctionCall(fnCall, traceId);
  371            if (event) {
  372              yield event;
  373            }
  374          }
  375
  376          for (const citation of getCitations(resp)) {
  377            this.pendingCitations.add(citation);
  378          }
  379
  380          // Check if response was truncated or stopped for various reasons
  381          const finishReason = resp.candidates?.[0]?.finishReason;
  382
  383          // This is the key change: Only yield 'Finished' if there is a finishReason.
  384          if (finishReason) {
  385            if (this.pendingCitations.size > 0) {
  386              yield {
  387                type: GeminiEventType.Citation,
  388                value: `Citations:\n${[...this.pendingCitations].sort().join('\n')}`,
  389              };
  390              this.pendingCitations.clear();
  391            }

  253  // A turn manages the agentic loop turn within the server context.
  254  export class Turn {
  255    private callCounter = 0;
  256
  257    readonly pendingToolCalls: ToolCallRequestInfo[] = [];
  258    private debugResponses: GenerateContentResponse[] = [];
  259    private pendingCitations = new Set<string>();
  260    private cachedResponseText: string | undefined = undefined;
  261    finishReason: FinishReason | undefined = undefined;
  262    private hasLoggedRagTrace = false;
  263
  264    constructor(
  265      private readonly chat: GeminiChat,
  266      private readonly prompt_id: string,
  267    ) {}
  268
  269    // The run method yields simpler events suitable for server logic
  270    async *run(
  271      modelConfigKey: ModelConfigKey,
  272      req: PartListUnion,
  273      signal: AbortSignal,
  274      options: {
  275        displayContent?: PartListUnion;
  276        role?: LlmRole;
  277        apiHistoryOverride?: Content[];
  278      } = {},
  279    ): AsyncGenerator<ServerGeminiStreamEvent> {
  280      const { displayContent, role = LlmRole.MAIN, apiHistoryOverride } = options;
  281      try {
  282        // Note: This assumes `sendMessageStream` yields events like
  283        // { type: StreamEventType.RETRY } or { type: StreamEventType.CHUNK, value: GenerateContentResponse }
  284        const responseStream = await this.chat.sendMessageStream(
  285          modelConfigKey,
  286          req,
  287          this.prompt_id,
  288          signal,
  289          role,
  290          displayContent,
  291          apiHistoryOverride,
  292        );
  293
  294        for await (const streamEvent of responseStream) {
  295          if (signal?.aborted) {
  296            yield { type: GeminiEventType.UserCancelled };
  297            return;
  298          }
  299
  300          // Handle the new RETRY event
  301          if (streamEvent.type === 'retry') {
  302            yield { type: GeminiEventType.Retry };
  303            continue; // Skip to the next event in the stream
  304          }
  305
  306          if (streamEvent.type === 'agent_execution_stopped') {
  307            yield {
  308              type: GeminiEventType.AgentExecutionStopped,
  309              value: { reason: streamEvent.reason },
  310            };
  311            return;
  312          }
  313
  314          if (streamEvent.type === 'agent_execution_blocked') {
  315            yield {
  316              type: GeminiEventType.AgentExecutionBlocked,
  317              value: { reason: streamEvent.reason },
  318            };
  319            continue;
  320          }
  321
  322          // Assuming other events are chunks with a `value` property
  323          const resp = streamEvent.value;
  324          if (!resp) continue; // Skip if there's no response body
  325
  326          // Log RAG trace if enabled (only once per turn to avoid log bloat on streams)
  327          if (
  328            !this.hasLoggedRagTrace &&
  329            this.chat.context.config.getLogRagSnippets?.()
  330          ) {
  331            let ragStatus: string | undefined;
  332            let snippets: RagSnippet[] | undefined;
  333
  334            if (
  335              typeof resp === 'object' &&
  336              resp !== null &&
  337              'metadata' in resp &&
  338              typeof resp.metadata === 'object' &&
  339              resp.metadata !== null
  340            ) {
  341              const metadata = resp.metadata as {
  342                ragStatus?: string;
  343                snippets?: RagSnippet[];
  344              };
  345              ragStatus = metadata.ragStatus;
  346              snippets = metadata.snippets;
  347            }
  348
  349            if (ragStatus || snippets) {
  350              ragLogger.log({
  351                sessionId: this.chat.context.config.getSessionId(),
  352                ragStatus: ragStatus ?? 'UNKNOWN',
  353                snippets: snippets ?? [],
  354              });
  355              this.hasLoggedRagTrace = true;
  356            }
  357          }
  358
  359          this.debugResponses.push(resp);
  360
  361          const traceId = resp.responseId;
  362
  363          const parts = resp.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts ?? [];
  364          for (const part of parts) {
  365            if (part.thought) {
  366              const thought = parseThought(part.text ?? '');
  367              yield {
  368                type: GeminiEventType.Thought,
  369                value: thought,
  370                traceId,
  371              };
  372            }
  373          }
  374
  375          const text = getResponseText(resp);
  376          if (text) {
  377            yield { type: GeminiEventType.Content, value: text, traceId };
  378          }
  379
  380          // Handle function calls (requesting tool execution)
  381          const functionCalls = resp.functionCalls ?? [];
  382          for (const fnCall of functionCalls) {
  383            const event = this.handlePendingFunctionCall(fnCall, traceId);
  384            if (event) {
  385              yield event;
  386            }
  387          }
  388
  389          for (const citation of getCitations(resp)) {
  390            this.pendingCitations.add(citation);
  391          }
02
L1facttools

02 · 超大工具结果在调度阶段落盘，取消也返回合法 functionResponse

源码事实

ToolExecutor 对超过阈值的文本写入项目临时目录并替换为截断文本；取消时若已有部分输出仍先截断/保存，再构造带 error 的 functionResponse，保持 Gemini 协议配对。

白话解释

工具被叫停也必须交一张正式回执；已经产生的大输出不会硬塞回上下文。

对自研 Harness 的含义

失败/取消不会破坏下一轮历史，且原始输出可从文件追查。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 250–272
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 273–297
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  299    private async createCancelledResult(
  300      call: ToolCall,
  301      reason: string,
  302      toolResult?: ToolResult,
  303    ): Promise<CancelledToolCall> {
  304      const errorMessage = `[Operation Cancelled] ${reason}`;
  305      const startTime = 'startTime' in call ? call.startTime : undefined;
  306
  307      if (!('tool' in call) || !('invocation' in call)) {
  308        // This should effectively never happen in execution phase, but we handle
  309        // it safely
  310        throw new Error('Cancelled tool call missing tool/invocation references');
  311      }
  312
  313      let responseParts: Part[] = [];
  314      let outputFile: string | undefined;
  315
  316      if (toolResult?.llmContent) {
  317        // Attempt to truncate and save output if we have content, even in cancellation case
  318        // This is to handle cases where the tool may have produced output before cancellation
  319        const { truncatedContent: output, outputFile: truncatedOutputFile } =
  320          await this.truncateOutputIfNeeded(call, toolResult?.llmContent);
  321
  322        outputFile = truncatedOutputFile;
  323        responseParts = convertToFunctionResponse(
  324          call.request.originalRequestName ?? call.request.name,
  325          call.request.callId,
  326          output,
  327          this.config.getActiveModel(),
  328          this.config,
  329        );
  330
  331        // Inject the cancellation error into the response object
  332        const mainPart = responseParts[0];
  333        if (mainPart?.functionResponse?.response) {
  334          const respObj = mainPart.functionResponse.response;
  335          respObj['error'] = errorMessage;
  336        }
  337      } else {
  338        responseParts = [
  339          {
  340            functionResponse: {
  341              id: call.request.callId,
  342              name: call.request.originalRequestName ?? call.request.name,
  343              response: { error: errorMessage },
  344            },
  345          },
  346        ];
  347      }
  348
  349      return {
  350        status: CoreToolCallStatus.Cancelled,
  351        request: call.request,
  352        response: {
  353          callId: call.request.callId,
  354          responseParts,
  355          display: toolResult?.display,
  356          resultDisplay: toolResult?.returnDisplay,
  357          error: undefined,
  358          errorType: undefined,
  359          outputFile,
  360          contentLength: JSON.stringify(responseParts).length,
  361        },
  362        tool: call.tool,
  363        invocation: call.invocation,
  364        durationMs: startTime ? Date.now() - startTime : undefined,
  365        startTime,
  366        endTime: Date.now(),
  367        outcome: call.outcome,
  368      };

06
DIMENSION · CONNECTORS

连接器、插件与 MCP

5 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factconnectors

01 · MCP 支持 stdio、Streamable HTTP、SSE fallback、OAuth、动态目录刷新和 progress

源码事实

McpClient 连接后发现 tools/prompts/resources 并注册，监听 listChanged；网络 transport 首选 HTTP，失败可回退 SSE，401 仅在显式 oauth.enabled 时发现/认证并重试；调用有 timeout、abort 与 progress token 路由。

白话解释

本地子进程和远程服务都能接；服务器换工具会热刷新，远程登录不是自动偷偷弹出，必须配置允许 OAuth。

对自研 Harness 的含义

连接器成熟，且认证意图边界清楚；热刷新仍需依赖 registry sort 与 policy validation。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 188–239
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 240–292
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · bef6119500b0 · 打开账本提交 ↗
packages/core/src/tools/mcp-client.ts:188–292固定提交 ↗
  188    async connect(): Promise<void> {
  189      if (this.status !== MCPServerStatus.DISCONNECTED) {
  190        throw new Error(
  191          `Can only connect when the client is disconnected, current state is ${this.status}`,
  192        );
  193      }
  194      this.updateStatus(MCPServerStatus.CONNECTING);
  195      try {
  196        this.client = await connectToMcpServer(
  197          this.clientVersion,
  198          this.serverName,
  199          this.serverConfig,
  200          this.debugMode,
  201          this.workspaceContext,
  202          this.cliConfig,
  203        );
  204
  205        this.registerNotificationHandlers();
  206
  207        const originalOnError = this.client.onerror;
  208        this.client.onerror = (error) => {
  209          if (this.status !== MCPServerStatus.CONNECTED) {
  210            return;
  211          }
  212          if (originalOnError) originalOnError(error);
  213          this.cliConfig.emitMcpDiagnostic(
  214            'error',
  215            `MCP ERROR (${this.serverName})`,
  216            error,
  217            this.serverName,
  218          );
  219          this.updateStatus(MCPServerStatus.DISCONNECTED);
  220        };
  221        this.updateStatus(MCPServerStatus.CONNECTED);
  222      } catch (error) {
  223        this.updateStatus(MCPServerStatus.DISCONNECTED);
  224        throw error;
  225      }
  226    }
  227
  228    /**
  229     * Discovers tools and prompts from the MCP server into the specified registries.
  230     */
  231    async discoverInto(
  232      cliConfig: McpContext,
  233      registries: RegistrySet,
  234    ): Promise<void> {
  235      this.assertConnected();
  236      this.registeredRegistries.add(registries);
  237
  238      const prompts = await this.fetchPrompts();
  239      const tools = await this.discoverTools(
  240        cliConfig,
  241        registries.toolRegistry.getMessageBus(),
  242      );
  243      const resources = await this.discoverResources();
  244      this.updateResourceRegistry(resources, registries.resourceRegistry);
  245
  246      if (prompts.length === 0 && tools.length === 0 && resources.length === 0) {
  247        throw new Error('No prompts, tools, or resources found on the server.');
  248      }
  249
  250      for (const prompt of prompts) {
  251        registries.promptRegistry.registerPrompt(prompt);
  252      }
  253      for (const tool of tools) {
  254        registries.toolRegistry.registerTool(tool);
  255      }
  256      registries.toolRegistry.sortTools();
  257
  258      // Validate MCP tool names in policy rules against discovered tools
  259      try {
  260        const discoveredToolNames = tools.map((t) => t.serverToolName);
  261        const policyRules = cliConfig.getPolicyEngine?.()?.getRules() ?? [];
  262        const warnings = validateMcpPolicyToolNames(
  263          this.serverName,
  264          discoveredToolNames,
  265          policyRules,
  266        );
  267        for (const warning of warnings) {
  268          coreEvents.emitFeedback('warning', warning);
  269        }
  270      } catch {
  271        // Policy engine may not be available in all contexts (e.g. tests).
  272        // Validation is best-effort; skip silently if unavailable.
  273      }
  274    }
  275
  276    /**
  277     * Unregisters registries so this client will no longer update them when it receives
  278     * list_changed notifications from the server.
  279     */
  280    removeRegistries(registries: RegistrySet): void {
  281      this.registeredRegistries.delete(registries);
  282    }
  283
  284    /**
  285     * Disconnects from the MCP server.
  286     */
  287    async disconnect(): Promise<void> {
  288      if (this.status !== MCPServerStatus.CONNECTED) {
  289        return;
  290      }
  291      for (const registries of this.registeredRegistries) {
  292        registries.toolRegistry.removeMcpToolsByServer(this.serverName);
  293        registries.promptRegistry.removePromptsByServer(this.serverName);
  294        registries.resourceRegistry.removeResourcesByServer(this.serverName);
  295      }
  296      this.updateStatus(MCPServerStatus.DISCONNECTING);
  297      const client = this.client;
  298      this.client = undefined;
  299      if (this.transport) {
  300        await this.transport.close();
  301      }
  302      if (client) {
  303        await client.close();
  304      }
  305      this.updateStatus(MCPServerStatus.DISCONNECTED);
  306    }
  307
  308    /**
  309     * Returns the current status of the client.
  310     */
  311    getStatus(): MCPServerStatus {
  312      return this.status;
  313    }
  314
  315    private updateStatus(status: MCPServerStatus): void {
  316      this.status = status;
  317      updateMCPServerStatus(this.serverName, status);
  318    }
  319
  320    private assertConnected(): void {
  321      if (this.status !== MCPServerStatus.CONNECTED) {
  322        throw new Error(
  323          `Client is not connected, must connect before interacting with the server. Current state is ${this.status}`,
  324        );
  325      }
  326    }
  327

02
L1factconnectors

02 · 扩展是能力包：MCP、policy/checker、context、commands、hooks、agents 与 skills 可成组热装卸

源码事实

ExtensionLoader 启动扩展时连接 MCP、刷新工具、注册 rules/checkers；批次完成后统一 refresh memory/system prompt/hooks/agent registry/skills。停止时按 source 移除政策并做同样刷新。

白话解释

扩展不是单个脚本，而是一箱可协同变化的工具、规则、记忆、钩子和 Agent。

对自研 Harness 的含义

扩展卸载能回收治理状态；批量刷新减少 prompt cache 抖动。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 31–69
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 70–110
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

packages/core/src/utils/extensionLoader.ts:31–110固定提交 ↗
   31    /**
   32     * Fully initializes all active extensions.
   33     *
   34     * Called within `Config.initialize`, which must already have an
   35     * McpClientManager, PromptRegistry, and GeminiChat set up.
   36     */
   37    async start(config: Config): Promise<void> {
   38      this.isStarting = true;
   39      try {
   40        if (!this.config) {
   41          this.config = config;
   42        } else {
   43          throw new Error('Already started, you may only call `start` once.');
   44        }
   45        await Promise.all(
   46          this.getExtensions()
   47            .filter((e) => e.isActive)
   48            .map(this.startExtension.bind(this)),
   49        );
   50      } finally {
   51        this.isStarting = false;
   52      }
   53    }
   54
   55    /**
   56     * Unconditionally starts an `extension` and loads all its MCP servers,
   57     * context, custom commands, etc. Assumes that `start` has already been called
   58     * and we have a Config object.
   59     *
   60     * This should typically only be called from `start`, most other calls should
   61     * go through `maybeStartExtension` which will only start the extension if
   62     * extension reloading is enabled and the `config` object is initialized.
   63     */
   64    protected async startExtension(extension: GeminiCLIExtension) {
   65      if (!this.config) {
   66        throw new Error('Cannot call `startExtension` prior to calling `start`.');
   67      }
   68      this.startingCount++;
   69      this.eventEmitter?.emit('extensionsStarting', {
   70        total: this.startingCount,
   71        completed: this.startCompletedCount,
   72      });
   73      try {
   74        await this.config.getMcpClientManager()!.startExtension(extension);
   75        await this.maybeRefreshGeminiTools(extension);
   76
   77        // Register policy rules and checkers
   78        if (extension.rules || extension.checkers) {
   79          const policyEngine = this.config.getPolicyEngine();
   80          if (extension.rules) {
   81            for (const rule of extension.rules) {
   82              policyEngine.addRule(rule);
   83            }
   84          }
   85          if (extension.checkers) {
   86            for (const checker of extension.checkers) {
   87              policyEngine.addChecker(checker);
   88            }
   89          }
   90        }
   91
   92        // Note: Context files are loaded only once all extensions are done
   93        // loading/unloading to reduce churn, see the `maybeRefreshMemories` call
   94        // below.
   95
   96        // TODO: Update custom command updating away from the event based system
   97        // and call directly into a custom command manager here. See the
   98        // useSlashCommandProcessor hook which responds to events fired here today.
   99      } finally {
  100        this.startCompletedCount++;
  101        this.eventEmitter?.emit('extensionsStarting', {
  102          total: this.startingCount,
  103          completed: this.startCompletedCount,
  104        });
  105        if (this.startingCount === this.startCompletedCount) {
  106          this.startingCount = 0;
  107          this.startCompletedCount = 0;
  108        }
  109        await this.maybeRefreshMemories();
  110      }
  111    }
  112
  113    private async maybeRefreshMemories(): Promise<void> {
  114      if (!this.config) {
  115        throw new Error(
  116          'Cannot refresh gemini memories prior to calling `start`.',
  117        );
  118      }
  119      if (
  120        !this.isStarting && // Don't refresh memories on the first call to `start`.
  121        this.startingCount === this.startCompletedCount &&
  122        this.stoppingCount === this.stopCompletedCount
  123      ) {
  124        // Wait until all extensions are done starting and stopping before we
  125        // reload memory, this is somewhat expensive and also busts the context
  126        // cache, we want to only do it once.
  127        await this.config.getMemoryContextManager()?.refresh();
  128        this.config.getGeminiClient().updateSystemInstruction();
  129        await this.config.getHookSystem()?.initialize();
  130        await this.config.getAgentRegistry().reload();
  131        await this.config.reloadSkills();
  132      }
  133    }
  134
  135    /**
  136     * Refreshes the gemini tools list if it is initialized and the extension has
  137     * any excludeTools settings.
  138     */
  139    private async maybeRefreshGeminiTools(
  140      extension: GeminiCLIExtension,
  141    ): Promise<void> {
  142      if (extension.excludeTools && extension.excludeTools.length > 0) {
  143        const geminiClient = this.config?.geminiClient;
  144        if (geminiClient?.isInitialized()) {
  145          await geminiClient.setTools();
  146        }
  147      }
  148    }
  149
  150    /**
  151     * If extension reloading is enabled and `start` has already been called,
  152     * then calls `startExtension` to include all extension features into the
  153     * program.
  154     */
  155    protected async maybeStartExtension(
  156      extension: GeminiCLIExtension,
  157    ): Promise<void> {
  158      if (this.config && this.config.getEnableExtensionReloading()) {
  159        await this.startExtension(extension);
  160      }
  161    }
  162
  163    /**
  164     * Unconditionally stops an `extension` and unloads all its MCP servers,
  165     * context, custom commands, etc. Assumes that `start` has already been called
  166     * and we have a Config object.
  167     *
  168     * Most calls should go through `maybeStopExtension` which will only stop the
  169     * extension if extension reloading is enabled and the `config` object is
  170     * initialized.
  171     */
  172    protected async stopExtension(extension: GeminiCLIExtension) {
  173      if (!this.config) {
  174        throw new Error('Cannot call `stopExtension` prior to calling `start`.');
  175      }
  176      this.stoppingCount++;
  177      this.eventEmitter?.emit('extensionsStopping', {
  178        total: this.stoppingCount,
  179        completed: this.stopCompletedCount,
  180      });
  181

03
L1factconnectors

03 · GEMINI.md/Memory 分 global、user-project、extension、project，并支持受信目录 JIT 加载

源码事实

memory discovery 分类别收集并串联；环境 memory 从 trusted root 向上到 git root；工具触达子目录时，只有目标处于 trusted root 才 JIT 搜索并按 inode/device 去重。

白话解释

常驻规章分层保存，走进更深目录时才加载当地规则；不受信目录不会因为读一个文件就注入它的说明。

对自研 Harness 的含义

兼顾大仓库按需上下文与 prompt injection 边界。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 383–417
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 418–454
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · bef6119500b0 · 打开账本提交 ↗
packages/core/src/utils/memoryDiscovery.ts:383–454固定提交 ↗
  383  export function getExtensionMemoryPaths(
  384    extensionLoader: ExtensionLoader,
  385  ): string[] {
  386    const extensionPaths = extensionLoader
  387      .getExtensions()
  388      .filter((ext) => ext.isActive)
  389      .flatMap((ext) => ext.contextFiles)
  390      .map((p) => toAbsolutePath(p));
  391
  392    // Deduplicate case-insensitively (so macOS/Windows don't keep two casings of
  393    // the same file) while preserving the first encountered casing for display.
  394    const seenKeys = new Set<string>();
  395    const unique: string[] = [];
  396    for (const p of extensionPaths) {
  397      const key = normalizePath(p);
  398      if (seenKeys.has(key)) continue;
  399      seenKeys.add(key);
  400      unique.push(p);
  401    }
  402    return unique.sort();
  403  }
  404
  405  export async function getEnvironmentMemoryPaths(
  406    trustedRoots: string[],
  407    boundaryMarkers: readonly string[] = ['.git'],
  408  ): Promise<string[]> {
  409    // Trusted Roots Upward Traversal (Parallelized)
  410    const traversalPromises = trustedRoots.map(async (root) => {
  411      const resolvedRoot = toAbsolutePath(root);
  412      const gitRoot = await findProjectRoot(resolvedRoot, boundaryMarkers);
  413      const ceiling = gitRoot ?? resolvedRoot;
  414      debugLogger.debug(
  415        '[DEBUG] [MemoryDiscovery] Loading environment memory for trusted root:',
  416        resolvedRoot,
  417        '(Stopping at',
  418        gitRoot
  419          ? `git root: ${ceiling})`
  420          : `trusted root: ${ceiling} — no git root found)`,
  421      );
  422      return findUpwardGeminiFiles(resolvedRoot, ceiling);
  423    });
  424
  425    const pathArrays = await Promise.all(traversalPromises);
  426
  427    const { paths: unique } = await deduplicatePathsByFileIdentity(
  428      pathArrays.flat(),
  429    );
  430    return unique.sort();
  431  }
  432
  433  export function categorizeAndConcatenate(
  434    paths: {
  435      global: string[];
  436      extension: string[];
  437      project: string[];
  438      userProjectMemory?: string[];
  439    },
  440    contentsMap: Map<string, GeminiFileContent>,
  441  ): HierarchicalMemory {
  442    const getConcatenated = (pList: string[]) =>
  443      concatenateInstructions(
  444        pList
  445          .map((p) => contentsMap.get(p))
  446          .filter((c): c is GeminiFileContent => !!c),
  447      );
  448
  449    return {
  450      global: getConcatenated(paths.global),
  451      extension: getConcatenated(paths.extension),
  452      project: getConcatenated(paths.project),
  453      userProjectMemory: getConcatenated(paths.userProjectMemory ?? []),
  454    };
  455  }
  456
  457  /**
  458   * Traverses upward from startDir to stopDir, finding all GEMINI.md variants.
  459   *
  460   * Files are ordered by directory level (root to leaf), with all filename
  461   * variants grouped together per directory.
  462   */
  463  async function findUpwardGeminiFiles(
  464    startDir: string,
  465    stopDir: string,
  466  ): Promise<string[]> {
  467    const upwardPaths: string[] = [];
  468    let currentDir = toAbsolutePath(startDir);
  469    const resolvedStopDirKey = normalizePath(stopDir);
  470    const geminiMdFilenames = getAllGeminiMdFilenames();
  471    const globalGeminiDirKey = normalizePath(path.join(homedir(), GEMINI_DIR));
  472
  473    debugLogger.debug(
  474      '[DEBUG] [MemoryDiscovery] Starting upward search from',
  475      currentDir,
  476      'stopping at',
  477      stopDir,
  478    );
  479
  480    while (true) {
  481      if (normalizePath(currentDir) === globalGeminiDirKey) {
  482        break;
  483      }
  484
  485      // Parallelize checks for all filename variants in the current directory
  486      const accessChecks = geminiMdFilenames.map(async (filename) => {
  487        const potentialPath = toAbsolutePath(path.join(currentDir, filename));
  488        try {
  489          await fs.access(potentialPath, fsSync.constants.R_OK);
  490          return potentialPath;
  491        } catch {
  492          return null;
  493        }
  494      });
  495
  496      const foundPathsInDir = (await Promise.all(accessChecks)).filter(
  497        (p): p is string => p !== null,
  498      );
  499
  500      upwardPaths.unshift(...foundPathsInDir);
  501
  502      const parentDir = path.dirname(currentDir);
  503      const currentKey = normalizePath(currentDir);
  504      if (currentKey === resolvedStopDirKey || currentDir === parentDir) {
  505        break;
  506      }
  507      currentDir = parentDir;
  508    }
  509    return upwardPaths;
  510  }
  511
  512  export async function loadJitSubdirectoryMemory(
  513    targetPath: string,
  514    trustedRoots: string[],
  515    alreadyLoadedPaths: Set<string>,
  516    alreadyLoadedIdentities?: Set<string>,
  517    boundaryMarkers: readonly string[] = ['.git'],
  518  ): Promise<MemoryLoadResult> {
  519    const resolvedTarget = toAbsolutePath(targetPath);
  520    let bestRoot: string | null = null;
  521    let bestRootKeyLength = -1;
  522
  523    // Find the deepest trusted root that contains the target path
  524    for (const root of trustedRoots) {
  525      if (isSubpath(root, targetPath)) {

04
L1factconnectors

04 · Skills 有明确覆盖顺序，workspace skills 受 folder trust 保护

源码事实

SkillManager 依次加载 builtin、extension、user、.agents alias、workspace，后者同名覆盖前者；未信任 workspace 时直接跳过项目 skills，并可由管理员全局禁用或按名禁用。

白话解释

越靠近项目的技能优先级越高，但项目没被信任前不会让它改 Agent 的做事方式。

对自研 Harness 的含义

skills 被正确视为可执行影响，而非普通文档。

  3. after
    回到调用者

   17  export class SkillManager {
   18    private skills: SkillDefinition[] = [];

   20    private adminSkillsEnabled = true;

   23     * Clears all discovered skills.
   24     */

   26      this.skills = [];
   27    }
   28
   29    /**
   30     * Resets session-scoped state (active skill names).
   31     */
   32    reset(): void {
   33      this.activeSkillNames.clear();
   34    }
   35
   36    /**
   37     * Sets administrative settings for skills.
   38     */
   39    setAdminSettings(enabled: boolean): void {
   40      this.adminSkillsEnabled = enabled;
   41    }
   42
   43    /**
   44     * Returns true if skills are enabled by the admin.
   45     */
   46    isAdminEnabled(): boolean {
   47      return this.adminSkillsEnabled;
   48    }
   49
   50    /**
   51     * Discovers skills from standard user and workspace locations, as well as extensions.
   52     * Precedence: Extensions (lowest) -> User -> Workspace (highest).
   53     */
   54    async discoverSkills(
   55      storage: Storage,
   56      extensions: GeminiCLIExtension[] = [],
   57      isTrusted: boolean = false,
   58    ): Promise<void> {
   59      this.clearSkills();
   60
   61      // 1. Built-in skills (lowest precedence)
   62      await this.discoverBuiltinSkills();
   63
   64      // 2. Extension skills
   65      for (const extension of extensions) {
   66        if (extension.isActive && extension.skills) {
   67          this.addSkillsWithPrecedence(extension.skills);
   68        }
   69      }
   70
   71      // 3. User skills
   72      const userSkills = await loadSkillsFromDir(Storage.getUserSkillsDir());
   73      this.addSkillsWithPrecedence(userSkills);
   74
   75      // 3.1 User agent skills alias (.agents/skills)
   76      const userAgentSkills = await loadSkillsFromDir(
   77        Storage.getUserAgentSkillsDir(),
   78      );
   79      this.addSkillsWithPrecedence(userAgentSkills);
   80
   81      // 4. Workspace skills (highest precedence)
   82      if (!isTrusted) {
   83        debugLogger.debug(
   84          'Workspace skills disabled because folder is not trusted.',
   85        );
   86        return;
   87      }
   88
   89      const projectSkills = await loadSkillsFromDir(
   90        storage.getProjectSkillsDir(),
   91      );
   92      this.addSkillsWithPrecedence(projectSkills);
   93
   94      // 4.1 Workspace agent skills alias (.agents/skills)
   95      const projectAgentSkills = await loadSkillsFromDir(
   96        storage.getProjectAgentSkillsDir(),
   97      );
   98      this.addSkillsWithPrecedence(projectAgentSkills);
   99    }
  100
  101    /**
  102     * Discovers built-in skills.
  103     */
  104    private async discoverBuiltinSkills(): Promise<void> {
  105      const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
  106      const builtinDir = path.join(__dirname, 'builtin');
  107
  108      const builtinSkills = await loadSkillsFromDir(builtinDir);
  109
  110      for (const skill of builtinSkills) {
  111        skill.isBuiltin = true;
  112      }
  113
  114      this.addSkillsWithPrecedence(builtinSkills);
  115    }
  116
  117    /**
  118     * Adds skills to the manager programmatically.
  119     */
  120    addSkills(skills: SkillDefinition[]): void {
  121      this.addSkillsWithPrecedence(skills);
  122    }
  123
  124    private addSkillsWithPrecedence(newSkills: SkillDefinition[]): void {
  125      const skillMap = new Map<string, SkillDefinition>(
  126        this.skills.map((s) => [s.name, s]),
  127      );
  128
  129      for (const newSkill of newSkills) {
  130        const existingSkill = skillMap.get(newSkill.name);
  131        if (existingSkill && existingSkill.location !== newSkill.location) {
  132          if (existingSkill.isBuiltin) {
  133            debugLogger.warn(
  134              `Skill "${newSkill.name}" from "${newSkill.location}" is overriding the built-in skill.`,
  135            );
  136          } else {
  137            coreEvents.emitFeedback(
  138              'warning',
  139              `Skill conflict detected: "${newSkill.name}" from "${newSkill.location}" is overriding the same skill from "${existingSkill.location}".`,
  140            );
  141          }
  142        }
  143        skillMap.set(newSkill.name, newSkill);
  144      }
  145
  146      this.skills = Array.from(skillMap.values());
  147    }
  148
  149    /**
  150     * Returns the list of enabled discovered skills.
  151     */
  152    getSkills(): SkillDefinition[] {
  153      return this.skills.filter((s) => !s.disabled);
  154    }
  155
  156    /**
  157     * Returns the list of enabled discovered skills that should be displayed in the UI.
  158     * This excludes built-in skills.
  159     */
  160    getDisplayableSkills(): SkillDefinition[] {
  161      return this.skills.filter((s) => !s.disabled && !s.isBuiltin);
  162    }
  163
  164    /**
  165     * Returns all discovered skills, including disabled ones.
  166     */
  167    getAllSkills(): SkillDefinition[] {
  168      return this.skills;
  169    }

05
L1factconnectors

05 · Before/AfterAgent hooks 可停止、阻断、注入上下文或要求清空后继续

源码事实

BeforeAgent 对每个 prompt_id 去重并可 stop/block/additionalContext；AfterAgent 只在最外层且没有 pending tools 时运行，可 stop、block、clearContext，并将 continue reason 作为新请求递归运行。

白话解释

钩子既能在开工前加背景/拦截，也能在收工时验收，不合格可清空现场后让 Agent 按理由重做。

对自研 Harness 的含义

适合策略与质量门，但递归 continuation 要有 turn 上限和 hook state 防重。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 153–201
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 202–252
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

packages/core/src/core/client.ts:153–252固定提交 ↗
  153    // Hook state to deduplicate BeforeAgent calls and track response for
  154    // AfterAgent
  155    private hookStateMap = new Map<
  156      string,
  157      {
  158        hasFiredBeforeAgent: boolean;
  159        cumulativeResponse: string;
  160        activeCalls: number;
  161        originalRequest: PartListUnion;
  162      }
  163    >();
  164
  165    private async fireBeforeAgentHookSafe(
  166      request: PartListUnion,
  167      prompt_id: string,
  168    ): Promise<BeforeAgentHookReturn> {
  169      let hookState = this.hookStateMap.get(prompt_id);
  170      if (!hookState) {
  171        hookState = {
  172          hasFiredBeforeAgent: false,
  173          cumulativeResponse: '',
  174          activeCalls: 0,
  175          originalRequest: request,
  176        };
  177        this.hookStateMap.set(prompt_id, hookState);
  178      }
  179
  180      // Increment active calls for this prompt_id
  181      // This is called at the start of sendMessageStream, so it acts as an entry
  182      // counter. We increment here, assuming this helper is ALWAYS called at
  183      // entry.
  184      hookState.activeCalls++;
  185
  186      if (hookState.hasFiredBeforeAgent) {
  187        return undefined;
  188      }
  189
  190      const hookOutput = await this.config
  191        .getHookSystem()
  192        ?.fireBeforeAgentEvent(partToString(request));
  193      hookState.hasFiredBeforeAgent = true;
  194
  195      if (hookOutput?.shouldStopExecution()) {
  196        return {
  197          type: GeminiEventType.AgentExecutionStopped,
  198          value: {
  199            reason: hookOutput.getEffectiveReason(),
  200            systemMessage: hookOutput.systemMessage,
  201          },
  202        };
  203      }
  204
  205      if (hookOutput?.isBlockingDecision()) {
  206        return {
  207          type: GeminiEventType.AgentExecutionBlocked,
  208          value: {
  209            reason: hookOutput.getEffectiveReason(),
  210            systemMessage: hookOutput.systemMessage,
  211          },
  212        };
  213      }
  214
  215      const additionalContext = hookOutput?.getAdditionalContext();
  216      if (additionalContext) {
  217        return { additionalContext };
  218      }
  219      return undefined;
  220    }
  221
  222    private async fireAfterAgentHookSafe(
  223      currentRequest: PartListUnion,
  224      prompt_id: string,
  225      turn?: Turn,
  226      stopHookActive: boolean = false,
  227    ): Promise<DefaultHookOutput | undefined> {
  228      const hookState = this.hookStateMap.get(prompt_id);
  229      // Only fire on the outermost call (when activeCalls is 1)
  230      if (!hookState || (hookState.activeCalls !== 1 && !stopHookActive)) {
  231        return undefined;
  232      }
  233
  234      if (turn && turn.pendingToolCalls.length > 0) {
  235        return undefined;
  236      }
  237
  238      const finalResponseText =
  239        hookState.cumulativeResponse ||
  240        turn?.getResponseText() ||
  241        '[no response text]';
  242      const finalRequest = hookState.originalRequest || currentRequest;
  243
  244      const hookOutput = await this.config
  245        .getHookSystem()
  246        ?.fireAfterAgentEvent(
  247          partToString(finalRequest),
  248          finalResponseText,
  249          stopHookActive,
  250        );
  251
  252      return hookOutput;
  253    }

07
DIMENSION · SECURITY

安全、审批与权限

5 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factsecurity

01 · PolicyEngine 按优先级匹配工具、参数、MCP 身份、annotations、模式、交互状态和 subagent

源码事实

ruleMatches 对稳定序列化参数、MCP 全限定名、tool annotations、approval mode、interactive/non-interactive 与 subagent 做组合匹配；规则/checker/hook checker 都按 priority 降序。

白话解释

政策可以精确到“哪个子 Agent 在非交互模式调用哪个 MCP 的哪个参数”，不只是允许/禁止 Bash。

对自研 Harness 的含义

企业控制力强，但规则冲突需要良好解释器和测试。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 49–121
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 122–195
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · bef6119500b0 · 打开账本提交 ↗
packages/core/src/policy/policy-engine.ts:49–195固定提交 ↗
   49  function isWildcardPattern(name: string): boolean {
   50    return name === '*' || name.includes('*');
   51  }
   52
   53  /**
   54   * Checks if a tool call matches a wildcard pattern.
   55   * Supports global (*) and the explicit MCP (*mcp_serverName_**) format.
   56   */
   57  function matchesWildcard(
   58    pattern: string,
   59    toolName: string,
   60    serverName: string | undefined,
   61  ): boolean {
   62    if (pattern === '*') {
   63      return true;
   64    }
   65
   66    if (pattern === `${MCP_TOOL_PREFIX}*`) {
   67      return serverName !== undefined;
   68    }
   69
   70    if (pattern.startsWith(MCP_TOOL_PREFIX) && pattern.endsWith('_*')) {
   71      const expectedServerName = pattern.slice(MCP_TOOL_PREFIX.length, -2);
   72      // 1. Must be an MCP tool call (has serverName)
   73      // 2. Server name must match
   74      // 3. Tool name must be properly qualified by that server
   75      if (serverName === undefined || serverName !== expectedServerName) {
   76        return false;
   77      }
   78      return toolName.startsWith(`${MCP_TOOL_PREFIX}${expectedServerName}_`);
   79    }
   80
   81    // Not a recognized wildcard pattern, fallback to exact match just in case
   82    return toolName === pattern;
   83  }
   84
   85  function ruleMatches(
   86    rule: PolicyRule | SafetyCheckerRule,
   87    toolCall: FunctionCall,
   88    stringifiedArgs: string | undefined,
   89    serverName: string | undefined,
   90    currentApprovalMode: ApprovalMode,
   91    nonInteractive: boolean,
   92    toolAnnotations?: Record<string, unknown>,
   93    subagent?: string,
   94  ): boolean {
   95    // Check if rule applies to current approval mode
   96    if (rule.modes && rule.modes.length > 0) {
   97      if (!rule.modes.includes(currentApprovalMode)) {
   98        return false;
   99      }
  100    }
  101
  102    // Check subagent if specified (only for PolicyRule, SafetyCheckerRule doesn't have it)
  103    if ('subagent' in rule && rule.subagent !== undefined) {
  104      if (rule.subagent !== subagent) {
  105        return false;
  106      }
  107    }
  108
  109    // Strictly enforce mcpName identity if the rule dictates it
  110    if (rule.mcpName !== undefined) {
  111      if (rule.mcpName === '*') {
  112        // Rule requires it to be ANY MCP tool
  113        if (serverName === undefined) return false;
  114      } else {
  115        // Rule requires it to be a specific MCP server
  116        if (serverName !== rule.mcpName) return false;
  117      }
  118    }
  119
  120    // Check tool name if specified
  121    if (rule.toolName !== undefined) {
  122      // Support wildcard patterns: "mcp_serverName_*" matches "mcp_serverName_anyTool"
  123      if (rule.toolName === '*') {
  124        // Match all tools
  125      } else if (isWildcardPattern(rule.toolName)) {
  126        if (
  127          !toolCall.name ||
  128          !matchesWildcard(rule.toolName, toolCall.name, serverName)
  129        ) {
  130          return false;
  131        }
  132      } else if (toolCall.name !== rule.toolName) {
  133        // If names don't match exactly, check for MCP short/full name mismatches
  134        let mcpMatch = false;
  135        if (serverName && toolCall.name) {
  136          // Case 1: Rule uses short name + mcpName -> match FQN tool call
  137          if (rule.mcpName && !isMcpToolName(rule.toolName)) {
  138            if (
  139              toolCall.name === formatMcpToolName(rule.mcpName, rule.toolName)
  140            ) {
  141              mcpMatch = true;
  142            }
  143          }
  144          // Case 2: Rule uses FQN -> match short tool call (qualified by serverName)
  145          if (!mcpMatch && isMcpToolName(rule.toolName)) {
  146            if (rule.toolName === formatMcpToolName(serverName, toolCall.name)) {
  147              mcpMatch = true;
  148            }
  149          }
  150        }
  151
  152        if (!mcpMatch) {
  153          return false;
  154        }
  155      }
  156    }
  157
  158    // Check annotations if specified
  159    if (rule.toolAnnotations) {
  160      if (!toolAnnotations) {
  161        return false;
  162      }
  163      for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(rule.toolAnnotations)) {
  164        if (toolAnnotations[key] !== value) {
  165          return false;
  166        }
  167      }
  168    }
  169
  170    // Check args pattern if specified
  171    if (rule.argsPattern) {
  172      // If rule has an args pattern but tool has no args, no match
  173      if (!toolCall.args) {
  174        return false;
  175      }
  176      // Use stable JSON stringification with sorted keys to ensure consistent matching
  177      if (
  178        stringifiedArgs === undefined ||
  179        !rule.argsPattern.test(stringifiedArgs)
  180      ) {
  181        return false;
  182      }
  183    }
  184
  185    // Check interactive if specified
  186    if ('interactive' in rule && rule.interactive !== undefined) {
  187      if (rule.interactive && nonInteractive) {
  188        return false;
  189      }
  190      if (!rule.interactive && !nonInteractive) {
  191        return false;
  192      }
  193    }
  194
  195    return true;
  196  }
  197
  198  export class PolicyEngine {
  199    private rules: PolicyRule[];
  200    private checkers: SafetyCheckerRule[];
  201    private hookCheckers: HookCheckerRule[];
  202    private readonly defaultDecision: PolicyDecision;
  203    private readonly nonInteractive: boolean;
  204    private readonly disableAlwaysAllow: boolean;
  205    private readonly checkerRunner?: CheckerRunner;
  206    private approvalMode: ApprovalMode;
  207    private readonly sandboxManager: SandboxManager;
  208
  209    constructor(config: PolicyEngineConfig = {}, checkerRunner?: CheckerRunner) {
  210      this.rules = (config.rules ?? []).sort(
  211        (a, b) => (b.priority ?? 0) - (a.priority ?? 0),
  212      );
  213      this.checkers = (config.checkers ?? []).sort(
  214        (a, b) => (b.priority ?? 0) - (a.priority ?? 0),
  215      );
  216      this.hookCheckers = (config.hookCheckers ?? []).sort(
  217        (a, b) => (b.priority ?? 0) - (a.priority ?? 0),
  218      );
  219
  220      // Validate rules
  221      for (const rule of this.rules) {
  222        if (rule.toolName === undefined || rule.toolName === '') {
  223          throw new Error(
  224            `Invalid policy rule: toolName is required. Use '*' for all tools. Rule source: ${rule.source || 'unknown'}`,
  225          );
  226        }
  227        if (rule.mcpName === '') {
  228          throw new Error(
  229            `Invalid policy rule: mcpName is required if specified (cannot be empty). Rule source: ${rule.source || 'unknown'}`,
  230          );
  231        }
  232        if (rule.subagent === '') {
  233          throw new Error(
  234            `Invalid policy rule: subagent is required if specified (cannot be empty). Rule source: ${rule.source || 'unknown'}`,
  235          );
  236        }
  237      }
  238
  239      // Validate checkers
  240      for (const checker of this.checkers) {
  241        if (checker.toolName === undefined || checker.toolName === '') {
  242          throw new Error(
  243            `Invalid safety checker rule: toolName is required. Use '*' for all tools. Checker source: ${checker.source || 'unknown'}`,
  244          );
  245        }
  246        if (checker.mcpName === '') {
  247          throw new Error(
  248            `Invalid safety checker rule: mcpName is required if specified (cannot be empty). Checker source: ${checker.source || 'unknown'}`,
  249          );
  250        }
  251      }
  252
  253      this.nonInteractive = config.nonInteractive ?? false;
  254      this.defaultDecision =
  255        config.defaultDecision ??
  256        (this.nonInteractive ? PolicyDecision.DENY : PolicyDecision.ASK_USER);
  257      this.disableAlwaysAllow = config.disableAlwaysAllow ?? false;
  258      this.checkerRunner = checkerRunner;
  259      this.approvalMode = config.approvalMode ?? ApprovalMode.DEFAULT;
  260      this.sandboxManager = config.sandboxManager ?? new NoopSandboxManager();
  261    }
  262
  263    /**
  264     * Update the current approval mode.
  265     */
  266    setApprovalMode(mode: ApprovalMode): void {

02
L1factsecurity

02 · 非交互默认拒绝，交互默认询问；危险命令强制 ASK，YOLO 例外

源码事实

PolicyEngine 未配置 defaultDecision 时，nonInteractive 为 DENY、交互为 ASK_USER；shell heuristic 将危险命令改为 ASK_USER，YOLO 保留原决定，已知安全命令可把 ASK 降为 ALLOW。

白话解释

没人看屏幕时不赌；有人在时先问。只有明确 YOLO 才允许危险命令绕过这层强制提问。

对自研 Harness 的含义

无头执行失败关闭，YOLO 是明确的高风险模式。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 253–255
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 256–260
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  284    private shouldDowngradeForRedirection(
  285      command: string,
  286      allowRedirection?: boolean,
  287    ): boolean {
  288      if (allowRedirection) return false;
  289      if (!hasRedirection(command)) return false;
  290
  291      // Do not downgrade (do not ask user) if in AUTO_EDIT or YOLO mode.
  292      // These modes trust the agent's actions (YOLO) or specific task (AUTO_EDIT).
  293      if (
  294        this.approvalMode === ApprovalMode.AUTO_EDIT ||
  295        this.approvalMode === ApprovalMode.YOLO
  296      ) {
  297        return false;
  298      }
  299
  300      return true;
  301    }

  325          );
  326          return PolicyDecision.ASK_USER;
  327        }

  329        if (
  330          this.sandboxManager.isKnownSafeCommand(parsedArgs) &&
  331          decision === PolicyDecision.ASK_USER

03
L1factsecurity

03 · Sandbox 默认不开启，但即使关闭仍净化环境变量

源码事实

factory 只有 sandbox.enabled 才按平台选择 Linux/Mac/Windows manager，否则用 NoopSandboxManager；Noop 不隔离进程，但会按安全配置 sanitize environment 后原样运行。

白话解释

防护罩不是默认扣上的；没扣罩子时至少会先从进程环境里清理不该传给子进程的东西。

对自研 Harness 的含义

必须评价为“可选强隔离”，不能写成“默认在沙箱中执行”。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 19–30
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 31–43
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   27    if (!options.modeConfig && options.policyManager && approvalMode) {

   38      }
   39      return new LocalSandboxManager(options);

   41

04
L1factsecurity

04 · 三平台使用真实 OS 隔离，并保护治理文件与 .env 类秘密

源码事实

Linux 使用 bubblewrap 并生成 seccomp BPF 禁 ptrace；macOS 用 sandbox-exec/Seatbelt profile；Windows 用 restricted token、Job Object、Low Integrity helper。共同模型保护 .git/.gitignore/.geminiignore，隐藏 .env/.env.*。

白话解释

开罩后不是靠模型自觉：Linux、macOS、Windows 各用系统级限制器，仓库规则和秘密文件另加保护。

对自研 Harness 的含义

跨平台安全面完整，但每个后端差异大，需各自做 denial 与路径逃逸回归。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 194–207
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 208–223
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  194  /**
  195   * Files that represent the governance or "constitution" of the repository
  196   * and should be write-protected in any sandbox.
  197   */
  198  export const GOVERNANCE_FILES = [
  199    { path: '.gitignore', isDirectory: false },
  200    { path: '.geminiignore', isDirectory: false },
  201    { path: '.git', isDirectory: true },
  202  ] as const;
  203
  204  /**
  205   * Files that contain sensitive secrets or credentials and should be
  206   * completely hidden (deny read/write) in any sandbox.
  207   */
  208  export const SECRET_FILES = [
  209    { pattern: '.env' },
  210    { pattern: '.env.*' },
  211  ] as const;
  212
  213  /**
  214   * Checks if a given file name matches any of the secret file patterns.
  215   */
  216  export function isSecretFile(fileName: string): boolean {
  217    return SECRET_FILES.some((s) => {
  218      if (s.pattern.endsWith('*')) {
  219        const prefix = s.pattern.slice(0, -1);
  220        return fileName.startsWith(prefix);
  221      }
  222      return fileName === s.pattern;
  223    });
  224  }
  225
  226  /**
  227   * Returns arguments for the Linux 'find' command to locate secret files.
  228   */
  229  export function getSecretFileFindArgs(): string[] {
  230    const args: string[] = ['('];
  231    SECRET_FILES.forEach((s, i) => {
  232      if (i > 0) args.push('-o');
  233      args.push('-name', s.pattern);
  234    });
  235    args.push(')');
  236    return args;
  237  }
  238
  239  /**
  240   * Finds all secret files in a directory up to a certain depth.
  241   * Default is shallow scan (depth 1) for performance.
  242   */
  243  export async function findSecretFiles(
  244    baseDir: string,
  245    maxDepth = 1,
  246  ): Promise<string[]> {
  247    const secrets: string[] = [];
  248    const skipDirs = new Set([
  249      'node_modules',
  250      '.git',
  251      '.venv',
  252      '__pycache__',
  253      'dist',
  254      'build',
  255      '.next',
  256      '.idea',
  257      '.vscode',
  258    ]);
  259
  260    async function walk(dir: string, depth: number) {
  261      if (depth > maxDepth) return;
  262      try {
  263        const entries = await fs.readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true });
  264        for (const entry of entries) {
  265          const fullPath = path.join(dir, entry.name);
  266          if (entry.isDirectory()) {
  267            if (!skipDirs.has(entry.name)) {
  268              await walk(fullPath, depth + 1);
  269            }
  270          } else if (entry.isFile()) {
  271            if (isSecretFile(entry.name)) {
  272              secrets.push(fullPath);
  273            }
  274          }
  275        }
  276      } catch {
  277        // Ignore read errors
  278      }
  279    }
  280
  281    await walk(baseDir, 1);
  282    return secrets;
  283  }
  284

  286   * A no-op implementation of SandboxManager that silently passes commands
  287   * through while applying environment sanitization.
  288   */
  289  export class NoopSandboxManager implements SandboxManager {
  290    constructor(private options?: GlobalSandboxOptions) {}
  291
  292    /**
  293     * Prepares a command by sanitizing the environment and passing through
  294     * the original program and arguments.

05
L1risksecurity

05 · 默认 sandbox mode 的 default 是可写 workspace，plan 才只读

源码事实

SandboxPolicyManager 内建 fallback 中 plan 为 readonly/network off，default 和 accepting_edits 为 writable/network off；YOLO 明确打开网络、写权限和 yolo 标志。

白话解释

即使开启沙箱，普通默认模式也允许改工作区；“开沙箱”不等于“只读”。

对自研 Harness 的含义

产品 UI 必须同时展示 sandbox enabled 与当前 mode，避免用户产生错误安全感。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 49–70
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 71–94
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  108    private isProtectedKey(key: string): boolean {
  109      return key === '__proto__' || key === 'constructor' || key === 'prototype';

  112    private loadConfig(): SandboxTomlSchemaType {
  113      if (!fs.existsSync(this.configPath)) {
  114        return SandboxPolicyManager.DEFAULT_CONFIG;
  115      }
  116
  117      try {
  118        const content = fs.readFileSync(this.configPath, 'utf8');
  119        return SandboxTomlSchema.parse(toml.parse(content));
  120      } catch (e) {
  121        debugLogger.error(`Failed to parse sandbox.toml: ${e}`);
  122        return SandboxPolicyManager.DEFAULT_CONFIG;
  123      }
  124    }
  125
  126    private saveConfig(): void {
  127      try {
  128        const dir = path.dirname(this.configPath);
  129        if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
  130          fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  131        }
  132        // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unsafe-type-assertion
  133        const content = toml.stringify(this.config as unknown as toml.JsonMap);
  134        fs.writeFileSync(this.configPath, content);
  135      } catch (e) {
  136        debugLogger.error(`Failed to save sandbox.toml: ${e}`);
  137      }
  138    }

  140    getModeConfig(
  141      mode: 'plan' | 'accepting_edits' | 'default' | 'yolo' | string,
  142    ): SandboxModeConfig {
  143      if (mode === 'yolo') {
  144        return {
  145          network: true,
  146          readonly: false,
  147          approvedTools: [],
  148          allowOverrides: true,
  149          yolo: true,
  150        };
  151      }
  152      if (mode === 'plan') return this.config.modes.plan;
  153      if (mode === 'accepting_edits' || mode === 'autoEdit')
  154        return this.config.modes.accepting_edits;
  155      if (mode === 'default') return this.config.modes.default;
  156
  157      // Default fallback
  158      return this.config.modes.default ?? this.config.modes.plan;
  159    }

DIMENSION · OBSERVABILITY

观测、日志与回放

3 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factobservability

01 · 会话记录是增量 JSONL，支持 rewind、metadata patch 和完整 checkpoint

源码事实

ChatRecordingService 逐行解析；$rewindTo 删除目标及以后消息，$set 可增量更新 metadata 或用 messages 数组重建 checkpoint，坏行被隔离忽略，缺关键 metadata 时回退 legacy parser。

白话解释

对话文件像事件日志：可以写“回到某一步”、只改元数据，也能偶尔写一张完整快照；一行坏了不拖垮整份会话。

对自研 Harness 的含义

支持 resume/rewind 与格式迁移，需持续测试 checkpoint 和增量事件的一致性。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 150–175
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 176–203
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  161        string,
  162        { isUser: boolean; isResumable: boolean }
  163      >();
  164      let isTrackingMemoryScratchpadFreshness = false;
  165      let memoryScratchpadIsStale = false;
  166      let firstUserMessageStr: string | undefined;
  167
  168      for await (const line of rl) {
  169        if (!line.trim()) continue;
  170        try {
  171          const record = JSON.parse(line) as unknown;
  172          if (isRewindRecord(record)) {
  173            if (isTrackingMemoryScratchpadFreshness) {
  174              memoryScratchpadIsStale = true;
  175            }
  176            const rewindId = record.$rewindTo;
  177            if (options?.metadataOnly) {
  178              const idx = messageIds.indexOf(rewindId);
  179              if (idx !== -1) {
  180                const removedIds = messageIds.splice(idx);
  181                for (const removedId of removedIds) {
  182                  messageKinds.delete(removedId);
  183                }
  184              } else {
  185                messageIds.length = 0;
  186                messageKinds.clear();
  187              }
  188            } else {
  189              let found = false;
  190              const idsToDelete: string[] = [];
  191              for (const [id] of messagesMap) {
  192                if (id === rewindId) found = true;
  193                if (found) idsToDelete.push(id);
  194              }
  195              if (found) {
  196                for (const id of idsToDelete) {
  197                  messagesMap.delete(id);
  198                }
  199              } else {
  200                messagesMap.clear();
  201              }
  202            }
  203          } else if (isMessageRecord(record)) {
  204            if (isTrackingMemoryScratchpadFreshness) {
  205              memoryScratchpadIsStale = true;
  206            }
  207            const id = record.id;
  208            const isUser = hasProperty(record, 'type') && record.type === 'user';
  209            const isResumable = isResumableMessageRecord(record);
  210            // Track message count and first user message
  211            if (options?.metadataOnly) {
  212              messageIds.push(id);
  213              messageKinds.set(id, { isUser, isResumable });
  214            }
  215            if (
  216              !firstUserMessageStr &&
  217              isUser &&
  218              hasProperty(record, 'content') &&
  219              record['content'] &&
  220              isResumable
  221            ) {
  222              // Basic extraction of first user message for display
  223              const rawContent = record['content'];
  224              if (Array.isArray(rawContent)) {
  225                firstUserMessageStr = rawContent
  226                  .map((p: unknown) => (isTextPart(p) ? p['text'] : ''))
  227                  .join('');
  228              } else if (typeof rawContent === 'string') {
  229                firstUserMessageStr = rawContent;
  230              }
  231            }
  232
  233            if (!options?.metadataOnly) {
  234              messagesMap.set(id, record);
  235              if (
  236                options?.maxMessages &&
  237                messagesMap.size > options.maxMessages
  238              ) {
  239                const firstKey = messagesMap.keys().next().value;
  240                if (typeof firstKey === 'string') messagesMap.delete(firstKey);
  241              }
  242            }
  243          } else if (isMetadataUpdateRecord(record)) {
  244            if (hasProperty(record.$set, 'memoryScratchpad')) {
  245              isTrackingMemoryScratchpadFreshness = Boolean(
  246                record.$set.memoryScratchpad,
  247              );
  248              memoryScratchpadIsStale = false;
  249            }
  250            if (
  251              hasProperty(record.$set, 'messages') &&
  252              Array.isArray(record.$set.messages)
  253            ) {
  254              // Checkpoint: clear and rebuild from the provided messages array
  255              messagesMap.clear();
  256              if (options?.metadataOnly) {
  257                messageIds.length = 0;
  258                messageKinds.clear();
  259              }
  260              for (const msg of record.$set.messages) {
  261                if (isMessageRecord(msg)) {
  262                  const id = msg.id;
  263                  const isUser = msg.type === 'user';
  264                  const isResumable = isResumableMessageRecord(msg);
  265
  266                  if (options?.metadataOnly) {
  267                    messageIds.push(id);
  268                    messageKinds.set(id, {
  269                      isUser,
  270                      isResumable,
  271                    });
  272                  } else {
  273                    messagesMap.set(id, msg);
  274                  }

02
L1factobservability

02 · OpenTelemetry 可导出到 GCP、OTLP HTTP/gRPC、文件或控制台

源码事实

telemetry SDK 同时构造 trace/log/metric exporter，支持 GCP 直出、OTLP HTTP/grpc+gzip、单文件和 console；NodeSDK 加载 HTTP instrumentation，并可周期监控内存和 event loop。

白话解释

既能接企业观测平台，也能只落本地文件；模型调用之外还能看到进程内存和事件循环卡顿。

对自研 Harness 的含义

生产可观测性完整，但凭据、提示内容与遥测开关需纳入隐私治理。

    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  319    } else if (telemetryOutfile) {
  320      spanExporter = new FileSpanExporter(telemetryOutfile);
  321      logExporter = new FileLogExporter(telemetryOutfile);
  322      metricReader = new PeriodicExportingMetricReader({
  323        exporter: new FileMetricExporter(telemetryOutfile),
  324        exportIntervalMillis: 10000,
  325      });
  326    } else {
  327      spanExporter = new ConsoleSpanExporter();
  328      logExporter = new ConsoleLogRecordExporter();
  329      metricReader = new PeriodicExportingMetricReader({
  330        exporter: new ConsoleMetricExporter(),
  331        exportIntervalMillis: 10000,
  332      });
  333    }
  334
  335    // Store processor references for manual flushing
  336    spanProcessor = new BatchSpanProcessor(spanExporter);
  337    logRecordProcessor = new BatchLogRecordProcessor(logExporter);
  338
  339    sdk = new NodeSDK({
  340      resource,
  341      spanProcessors: [spanProcessor],
  342      logRecordProcessors: [logRecordProcessor],
  343      metricReader,
  344      instrumentations: [new HttpInstrumentation()],
  345    });
  346
  347    try {
  348      sdk.start();
  349      if (config.getDebugMode()) {
  350        debugLogger.log('OpenTelemetry SDK started successfully.');
  351      }
  352      activeTelemetryEmail = credentials?.client_email;
  353      initializeMetrics(config);
  354
  355      // Start memory monitoring if interval is specified via environment variable
  356      const monitorInterval = process.env['GEMINI_MEMORY_MONITOR_INTERVAL'];
  357      debugLogger.log(
  358        `[TELEMETRY] GEMINI_MEMORY_MONITOR_INTERVAL: ${monitorInterval}`,
  359      );
  360      if (monitorInterval) {
  361        const intervalMs = parseInt(monitorInterval, 10);
  362        if (!isNaN(intervalMs) && intervalMs > 0) {
  363          startGlobalMemoryMonitoring(config, intervalMs);
  364          startGlobalEventLoopMonitoring(config, intervalMs);
  365          // Disable enhanced monitoring (rate limiting/high water mark) in tests
  366          // to ensure we get regular snapshots regardless of growth.
  367          const monitor = getMemoryMonitor();
  368          if (monitor) {
  369            monitor.setEnhancedMonitoring(false);
  370          }
  371        }
  372      }
  373
  374      telemetryInitialized = true;
  375      void flushTelemetryBuffer();
  376    } catch (error) {
  377      debugLogger.error('Error starting OpenTelemetry SDK:', error);
  378    }
  379
  380    // Note: We don't use process.on('exit') here because that callback is synchronous
  381    // and won't wait for the async shutdownTelemetry() to complete.
  382    // Instead, telemetry shutdown is handled in runExitCleanup() in cleanup.ts
  383    process.on('SIGTERM', () => {
  384      // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-floating-promises
  385      shutdownTelemetry(config);
  386    });
  387    process.on('SIGINT', () => {
  388      // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-floating-promises
  389      shutdownTelemetry(config);

03
L3factobservability

03 · 大型 TypeScript monorepo，测试面广，Apache-2.0

源码事实

固定提交约 2,144 个 TypeScript 文件，机械统计 892 个 *.test.ts/tsx；根许可证是 Apache License 2.0。

白话解释

这是一套带 CLI、core、SDK、ACP/A2A、策略和平台沙箱的系统，不是单文件 demo。

对自研 Harness 的含义

架构可借鉴性高，但新旧上下文路径和多前端会扩大回归矩阵。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–13
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 14–28
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

边界 / 风险
  • 文件数是固定 checkout 的机械统计，不等于测试覆盖率。

09
DIMENSION · COLLABORATION

协作与子 Agent

2 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factcollaboration

01 · 子 Agent 统一为可订阅、可重放、可中止的 AgentProtocol

源码事实

AgentSession 包装 send/subscribe/abort/events，并把一次 stream 限定在 agent_start→agent_end；它先订阅再回放历史，处理 setup 期间 early events，支持按 eventId 或 streamId 重接。

白话解释

无论子 Agent 在本机还是远程，上层看到的都是一条带编号、能续看的事件流。

对自研 Harness 的含义

UI、SDK 与 A2A server 可共享协议，断线恢复不必理解每种 executor。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 14–40
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 41–69
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。
