packages/core/src/agent/agent-session.ts:14–69固定提交 ↗
   14  /**
   15   * AgentSession is a wrapper around AgentProtocol that provides a more
   16   * convenient API for consuming agent activity as an AsyncIterable.
   17   */
   18  export class AgentSession implements AgentProtocol {
   19    private _protocol: AgentProtocol;
   20
   21    constructor(protocol: AgentProtocol) {
   22      this._protocol = protocol;
   23    }
   24
   25    async send(payload: AgentSend): Promise<{ streamId: string | null }> {
   26      return this._protocol.send(payload);
   27    }
   28
   29    subscribe(callback: (event: AgentEvent) => void): Unsubscribe {
   30      return this._protocol.subscribe(callback);
   31    }
   32
   33    async abort(): Promise<void> {
   34      return this._protocol.abort();
   35    }
   36
   37    get events(): readonly AgentEvent[] {
   38      return this._protocol.events;
   39    }
   40
   41    /**
   42     * Sends a payload to the agent and returns an AsyncIterable that yields
   43     * events for the resulting stream.
   44     *
   45     * @param payload The payload to send to the agent.
   46     */
   47    async *sendStream(payload: AgentSend): AsyncIterable<AgentEvent> {
   48      const result = await this._protocol.send(payload);
   49      const streamId = result.streamId;
   50
   51      if (streamId === null) {
   52        return;
   53      }
   54
   55      yield* this.stream({ streamId });
   56    }
   57
   58    /**
   59     * Returns an AsyncIterable that yields events from the agent session,
   60     * optionally replaying events from history or reattaching to an existing stream.
   61     *
   62     * @param options Options for replaying or reattaching to the event stream.
   63     */
   64    async *stream(
   65      options: {
   66        eventId?: string;
   67        streamId?: string;
   68      } = {},
   69    ): AsyncIterable<AgentEvent> {
   70      let resolve: (() => void) | undefined;
   71      let next = new Promise<void>((res) => {
   72        resolve = res;
   73      });
   74
   75      let eventQueue: AgentEvent[] = [];
   76      const earlyEvents: AgentEvent[] = [];
   77      let done = false;
   78      let trackedStreamId = options.streamId;
   79      let started = false;
   80      let agentActivityStarted = false;
   81
   82      const queueVisibleEvent = (event: AgentEvent): void => {
   83        if (trackedStreamId && event.streamId !== trackedStreamId) {
   84          return;
   85        }
   86
   87        if (!agentActivityStarted) {
   88          if (event.type !== 'agent_start') {
   89            return;
   90          }
   91          trackedStreamId = event.streamId;
   92          agentActivityStarted = true;
   93        }
   94
   95        if (!trackedStreamId) {
   96          return;
   97        }
   98
   99        eventQueue.push(event);
  100        if (event.type === 'agent_end' && event.streamId === trackedStreamId) {
  101          done = true;
  102        }
  103      };
  104
  105      // 1. Subscribe early to avoid missing any events that occur during replay setup
  106      const unsubscribe = this._protocol.subscribe((event) => {
  107        if (done) return;
  108
  109        if (!started) {
  110          earlyEvents.push(event);
  111          return;
  112        }
  113
  114        queueVisibleEvent(event);
  115
  116        const currentResolve = resolve;
  117        next = new Promise<void>((r) => {
  118          resolve = r;
  119        });
  120        currentResolve?.();
  121      });
  122
  123      try {
  124        const currentEvents = this._protocol.events;
  125        let replayStartIndex = -1;
  126
  127        if (options.eventId) {
  128          const index = currentEvents.findIndex((e) => e.id === options.eventId);
  129          if (index === -1) {
  130            throw new Error(`Unknown eventId: ${options.eventId}`);
  131          }
  132
  133          const resumeEvent = currentEvents[index];
  134          trackedStreamId = resumeEvent.streamId;
  135          const firstAgentStartIndex = currentEvents.findIndex(
  136            (event) =>
  137              event.type === 'agent_start' && event.streamId === trackedStreamId,
  138          );
  139
  140          if (resumeEvent.type === 'agent_end') {

02
L1limitationcollaboration

02 · Local 子 Agent 支持后台执行和取消，但同一 protocol 实例不允许并发 stream

源码事实

LocalSubagentProtocol 在 send(message) 时若已有 activeStreamId 直接报错；它用 setTimeout 后台启动，独立 AbortController，取消时丢弃 partial output 并返回空 aborted result。

白话解释

一个子 Agent 会话一次只接一单；主线程能先拿到 streamId，不必等它做完，但不能同时塞第二单。

对自研 Harness 的含义

并发应通过多个 Agent 实例而非重入同一实例，取消的部分成果不会自动保留。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 69–81
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 82–95
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · bef6119500b0 · 打开账本提交 ↗
packages/core/src/agents/local-subagent-protocol.ts:69–95固定提交 ↗
   69  class LocalSubagentProtocol implements AgentProtocol {
   70    private _events: AgentEvent[] = [];
   71    private _subscribers = new Set<(event: AgentEvent) => void>();
   72    private _streamId: string = randomUUID();
   73    private _eventCounter = 0;
   74    private _agentStartEmitted = false;
   75    private _agentEndEmitted = false;
   76    private _activeStreamId: string | undefined;
   77    private _abortController = new AbortController();
   78
   79    // Result promise wiring — re-created per stream in _beginNewStream()
   80    private _resultResolve!: (output: OutputObject) => void;
   81    private _resultReject!: (err: unknown) => void;
   82    private _resultPromise: Promise<OutputObject> | undefined;
   83
   84    // Buffered config from send({update})
   85    private _bufferedConfig: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   86
   87    constructor(
   88      private readonly definition: LocalAgentDefinition,
   89      private readonly context: AgentLoopContext,
   90      // Required for API parity across protocol constructors (local, remote, legacy)
   91      _messageBus: MessageBus,
   92      private readonly _rawActivityCallback?: (
   93        activity: SubagentActivityEvent,
   94      ) => void,
   95    ) {}
   96
   97    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   98    // AgentProtocol interface
   99    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  100
  101    get events(): readonly AgentEvent[] {
  102      return this._events;
  103    }
  104
  105    subscribe(callback: (event: AgentEvent) => void): Unsubscribe {
  106      this._subscribers.add(callback);
  107      return () => {
  108        this._subscribers.delete(callback);
  109      };
  110    }
  111
  112    async send(payload: AgentSend): Promise<{ streamId: string | null }> {
  113      if ('update' in payload && payload.update) {
  114        // Buffer config for use when message send arrives
  115        if (payload.update.config) {
  116          this._bufferedConfig = {
  117            ...this._bufferedConfig,
  118            ...payload.update.config,
  119          };
  120        }
  121        return { streamId: null };
  122      }
  123
  124      if ('message' in payload && payload.message) {
  125        if (this._activeStreamId) {
  126          throw new Error(
  127            'LocalSubagentProtocol.send() cannot be called while a stream is active.',
  128          );
  129        }
  130
  131        // Extract query text from the message ContentParts
  132        const queryText = payload.message.content
  133          .filter((p): p is ContentPart & { type: 'text' } => p.type === 'text')
  134          .map((p) => p.text)
  135          .join('');
  136
  137        // Only include 'query' in params when the message text is non-empty,
  138        // so that callers that pass all fields via update.config are not affected.
  139        const params: AgentInputs = {
  140          ...this._bufferedConfig,
  141          ...(queryText.length > 0 ? { query: queryText } : {}),
  142        };
  143        this._bufferedConfig = {};
  144
  145        this._beginNewStream();
  146        const streamId = this._streamId;
  147
  148        // Schedule execution in a macrotask so send() resolves before agent_start
  149        setTimeout(() => {
  150          void this._runExecutionInBackground(params);
  151        }, 0);
  152
  153        return { streamId };
  154      }
  155
  156      // action and elicitations are not supported
  157      return { streamId: null };
  158    }
  159
  160    async abort(): Promise<void> {
  161      this._abortController.abort();
  162    }
  163
  164    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  165    // Protocol-specific: result access
  166    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
